### [1] [ReactiveGWM: Steering NPC in Reactive Game World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15256)
*Zeqing Wang,Danze Chen,Zhaohu Xing,Zizhao Tong,Yinhan Zhang,Xingyi Yang,Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReactiveGWM是一种反应式游戏世界模型，通过解耦玩家控制与NPC行为，实现零样本策略迁移——无需重新训练即可将NPC交互能力插入不同游戏的现成世界模型中。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型仅从玩家视角模拟环境，将NPC视为背景像素，无法捕捉玩家与NPC之间的真实交互，缺乏动作引发的NPC反应建模能力，本质上是被动的视频渲染器而非真正的模拟引擎。

Method: 将玩家控制与NPC行为显式解耦：玩家动作通过轻量级加性偏置注入扩散主干；高层级NPC响应（如进攻、控制、防御）通过交叉注意力模块实现。该模块学习与游戏无关的交互逻辑表示，支持零样本策略迁移到不同游戏的现成世界模型中。

Result: 在两个街霸游戏上的评估表明，ReactiveGWM在保持细粒度玩家可控性的同时，实现了鲁棒的、与提示对齐的NPC策略遵循。

Conclusion: ReactiveGWM为可扩展的、富含策略的NPC交互铺平了道路，无需领域特定的重新训练即可解锁可操控的NPC交互能力。

Original Abstract: Current game world models simulate environments from a subjective, player-centric perspective. However, by treating the Non-Player Character (NPC) merely as background pixels, these models cannot capture interactions between the player and NPC. In that sense, they act as passive video renderers rather than real simulation engines, lacking the physical understanding needed to model action-induced NPC reactivities. We introduce ReactiveGWM, a reactive game world model that synthesizes dynamic interactions between the player and NPC. Instead of entangling all interaction dynamics, ReactiveGWM explicitly decouples player controls from NPC behaviors. Player actions are injected into the diffusion backbone via a lightweight additive bias, while high-level NPC responses (e.g., Offense, Control, Defense) are grounded through cross-attention modules. Crucially, these modules learn a game-agnostic representation of interactive logic. This enables zero-shot strategy transfer: our learned modules can be plugged directly into off-the-shelf, unannotated world models of different games. This instantly unlocks steerable NPC interactions without any domain-specific retraining. Evaluated on two Street Fighter games, ReactiveGWM maintains fine-grain player controllability while achieving robust, prompt-aligned NPC strategy adherence, paving the way for scalable, strategy-rich interaction with the NPC.

中文摘要翻译: 当前游戏世界模型从主观的玩家中心视角模拟环境。然而，将非玩家角色（NPC）仅视为背景像素，这些模型无法捕捉玩家与NPC之间的交互。从这种意义上说，它们扮演的是被动视频渲染器的角色，而非真正的模拟引擎，缺乏建模动作引发的NPC反应所需的物理理解能力。我们提出ReactiveGWM，一个反应式游戏世界模型，用于合成玩家与NPC之间的动态交互。ReactiveGWM并非纠缠所有交互动态，而是显式地将玩家控制与NPC行为解耦。玩家动作通过轻量级加性偏置注入扩散主干，而高层级NPC响应（如进攻、控制、防御）则通过交叉注意力模块进行接地。关键在于，这些模块学习了一种与游戏无关的交互逻辑表示。这使得零样本策略迁移成为可能：我们学习的模块可以直接插入不同游戏中现成的、未标注的世界模型。这无需任何领域特定的重新训练即可立即解锁可操控的NPC交互。在两个街霸游戏上的评估表明，ReactiveGWM在保持细粒度玩家可控性的同时，实现了鲁棒的、与提示对齐的NPC策略遵循，为可扩展的、富含策略的NPC交互铺平了道路。

</details>


### [2] [Deep Pre-Alignment for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15300)
*Tianyu Yu,Kechen Fang,Zihao Wan,Kaidong Zhang,Yicheng Zhang,Jun Song,Bo Zheng,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Deep Pre-Alignment (DPA)架构，用小型VLM替换标准ViT编码器作为感知器，实现视觉特征与文本空间的深度对齐，在多个规模和模型家族上提升多模态性能并减少语言能力遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM中ViT编码器输出的视觉特征与LLM文本空间存在对齐差距，导致LLM早期层被迫浪费深度在浅层的模态对齐上，而非用于深度理解和复杂推理。

Method: 提出DPA架构，用一个小型VLM替换标准ViT编码器作为感知器（perceiver），在进入目标LLM之前就完成视觉特征与文本空间的深度预对齐。

Result: 在4B参数规模上，DPA在8个多模态基准上超过基线1.9分；在32B规模上差距扩大至3.0分。同时，在3个文本基准上语言能力遗忘减少32.9%。该增益在Qwen3和LLaMA 3.2等不同LLM家族上一致。

Conclusion: DPA是一种通用且高效的架构改进方案，仅需模块化替换视觉编码器并增加少量计算开销，即可无缝升级现有VLM，实现更好的对齐效果和更少的能力遗忘。

Original Abstract: Most Vision Language Models (VLMs) directly map outputs from ViT encoders to the LLM via a lightweight projector. While effective, recent analysis suggests this architecture suffers from an alignment challenge: visual features remain distant from the text space in the initial layers of the LLM, forcing the model to waste critical depth~\cite{zhang-etal-2024-investigating,artzy-schwartz-2024-attend} on superficial modality alignment rather than deep understanding and complex reasoning. In this work, we propose Deep Pre-Alignment (DPA), a novel architecture that replaces the standard ViT encoder with a small VLM as perceiver, ensuring visual features are deeply aligned with the text space of the target large language model. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of DPA. On the 4B parameter scale, DPA outperforms baselines by 1.9 points across 8 multimodal benchmarks, with gains widening to 3.0 points at the 32B scale. Moreover, by offloading alignment to the perceiver, DPA achieves a 32.9\% reduction in language capability forgetting over 3 text benchmarks. We further demonstrate that these gains are consistent across different LLM families including Qwen3 and LLaMA 3.2, highlighting the generality of our approach. Beyond performance, DPA also offers a seamless upgrade path for current VLM development, requiring only a modular replacement for the visual encoder with marginal computation overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数视觉语言模型（VLM）通过轻量级投影器将ViT编码器的输出直接映射到大型语言模型（LLM）。虽然这种方法有效，但近期分析表明该架构存在对齐挑战：视觉特征在LLM初始层中与文本空间仍相距甚远，迫使模型将关键深度浪费在浅层的模态对齐上，而非用于深度理解与复杂推理。本文提出深度预对齐（DPA）架构，用一个小型VLM替代标准ViT编码器作为感知器，确保视觉特征与目标LLM的文本空间实现深度对齐。全面的实验证明了DPA的有效性：在4B参数规模上，DPA在8个多模态基准上超过基线1.9分；在32B规模上优势扩大至3.0分。此外，通过将对齐任务卸载至感知器，DPA在3个文本基准上将语言能力遗忘降低了32.9%。我们进一步证明这些增益在Qwen3和LLaMA 3.2等不同LLM家族中保持一致，凸显了方法的普适性。除性能提升外，DPA还为当前VLM开发提供了无缝升级路径，仅需模块化替换视觉编码器，且计算开销极小。

</details>


### [3] [One Pass Is Not Enough: Recursive Latent Refinement for Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15309)
*Mehdi Esmaeilzadeh,Alexia Jolicoeur-Martineau,Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RTM，通过递归细化替换风格生成器中的单步潜在映射，在不牺牲FID的前提下显著提升生成模型的精度和召回率，实现更优的多样性和覆盖度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主流评价指标FID已趋饱和，且将样本保真度与模式覆盖度混为一谈，导致模型即使发生模式坍塌仍可获得低FID；因此需要更关注多样性和覆盖度的提升，而非仅仅优化FID。

Method: 提出RTM，在风格化生成器中用迭代递归细化过程替代原有的单步潜在映射，并结合隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）进行训练，IMLE天然优化模式覆盖度，从而同时提升生成质量的多样性与保真度。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CelebA-HQ 256×256及九个少样本基准上，RTM在保持竞争性FID的同时达到了最高的精度和召回率；同时RTM也能改进StyleGAN2和StyleGAN2-ADA在CIFAR-10和AFHQ-v1 512×512上的表现，证明其收益不限于IMLE。

Conclusion: 递归细化能够同时提升生成质量和多样性，避免了流匹配等方法以牺牲覆盖度为代价换取低FID的折中，为超越饱和的FID指标提供了有效途径。

Original Abstract: Despite remarkable progress, image generation is far from solved. The dominant metric, FID, conflates sample fidelity with mode coverage and is close to being saturated. Yet a model can still exhibit mode collapse while achieving a low FID, since a handful of sharp, near-duplicate images can outscore a model that faithfully covers the full data distribution. We argue that precision and recall are essential complements to FID, and that because FID is already saturated, the more meaningful goal is to improve diversity and coverage. Achieving high recall requires a model that explicitly prioritizes mode coverage, unlike most generative models, which optimize sample fidelity. We introduce RTM, which replaces the single-pass latent mapping in style-based generators with an iterative refinement process, and show that this consistently improves both quality and diversity. Integrated with Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), which optimizes mode coverage by design, RTM achieves the highest precision and recall among current state-of-the-art approaches while maintaining competitive FID, with improvements across CIFAR-10, CelebA-HQ at 256x256, and nine few-shot benchmarks. RTM also improves StyleGAN2 and StyleGAN2-ADA on CIFAR-10 and AFHQ-v1 at 512x512, demonstrating that the benefit is not specific to IMLE. Unlike flow-matching baselines that achieve competitive FID at the expense of coverage, recursive refinement improves both quality and diversity simultaneously.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管取得了显著进展，图像生成问题仍未得到完全解决。主流评价指标FID将样本保真度与模式覆盖度混为一谈，且已接近饱和。然而，一个模型即使出现模式坍塌（mode collapse）仍可能获得低FID，因为少量清晰且近乎重复的图像，其得分可能高于一个忠实覆盖完整数据分布的模型。我们认为精度（precision）和召回率（recall）是FID的重要补充指标，并且由于FID已经饱和，更有意义的目标是提升多样性和覆盖度。实现高召回率需要模型明确优先考虑模式覆盖，这与大多数优化样本保真度的生成模型不同。我们引入了RTM，该方法用迭代细化（iterative refinement）过程替代风格化生成器中的单步潜在映射，实验表明这能持续提升质量和多样性。RTM与隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）集成，IMLE天然地优化模式覆盖度，在保持竞争性FID的同时，RTM在CIFAR-10、CelebA-HQ（256×256）以及九个少样本基准上取得了当前最先进方法中最高精度和召回率。RTM还能改进StyleGAN2和StyleGAN2-ADA在CIFAR-10和AFHQ-v1（512×512）上的表现，表明其收益并非IMLE所独有。与那些以牺牲覆盖度为代价换取竞争性FID的流匹配（flow-matching）基线不同，递归细化能同时提升生成质量和多样性。

</details>


### [4] [COPRA: Conditional Parameter Adaptation with Reinforcement Learning for Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15325)
*Darryl Cherian Jacob,Xinyu Liu,Kai Wang,Pan He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COPRA提出了一种条件参数自适应框架，通过为每个视频片段生成输入特定的参数更新，动态适配冻结的视觉-语言模型，解决了视频异常检测中训练与推理在数据分布和模型配置上的不一致问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于VLM的VAD方法存在两个根本性不匹配：(1) 依赖静态后训练适配，在分布偏移（如未见环境或异常类型）下泛化能力有限；(2) 训练时使用稀疏帧，推理时使用密集采样的短片段，造成训练与测试的不一致。

Method: COPRA是一种条件参数自适应框架，不依赖固定提示或共享参数更新，而是为每个视频片段生成输入特定的参数更新，从而在训练和推理过程中动态适配冻结的VLM。

Result: 在标准VAD基准上表现优异，在域内和跨域设置中均持续优于静态基线方法；此外，COPRA还能泛化到未见任务如多项选择视频问答和密集描述。

Conclusion: COPRA是一种有效的权重空间生成框架，支持可扩展、自适应且上下文感知的视频理解。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance in video anomaly detection (VAD) while providing interpretable predictions. However, existing VLM-based VAD methods suffer from a fundamental mismatch between training and inference in both data distribution and model configuration. First, most approaches rely on static post-training adaptation, limiting generalization under distribution shifts such as unseen environments or anomaly types. Second, they train VLMs on sparse frames from long videos, but perform inference on densely sampled short segments, creating inconsistencies between training and testing. To address these limitations, we propose COPRA, a conditional parameter adaptation framework for VLM-based VAD. Instead of fixed prompts or shared parameter updates, COPRA generates input-specific parameter updates to dynamically adapt a frozen VLM for each video segment during both training and inference. Experiments show strong performance on standard VAD benchmarks, consistently outperforming static baselines in both in-domain and cross-domain settings. Moreover, COPRA generalizes beyond VAD to unseen tasks such as multiple-choice Video Question Answering and Dense Captioning. These results highlight COPRA as an effective weight-space generation framework for scalable, adaptive, and context-aware video understanding. The code will be released at https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/COPRA

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）在视频异常检测（VAD）中展现出强大性能，同时提供可解释的预测。然而，现有的基于VLM的VAD方法在数据分布和模型配置上存在训练与推理之间的根本性不匹配。首先，大多数方法依赖静态的后训练适配，在分布偏移（如未见环境或异常类型）下泛化能力有限。其次，它们使用长视频中的稀疏帧训练VLM，但在推理时对密集采样的短片段进行预测，造成训练与测试之间的不一致。为解决这些局限，我们提出了COPRA，一种用于基于VLM的VAD的条件参数自适应框架。COPRA不依赖固定提示或共享参数更新，而是为每个视频片段生成输入特定的参数更新，在训练和推理过程中动态适配冻结的VLM。实验表明，COPRA在标准VAD基准上表现优异，在域内和跨域设置中均持续优于静态基线方法。此外，COPRA还能泛化到VAD之外的未见任务，如多项选择视频问答和密集描述。这些结果突出了COPRA作为一种有效的权重空间生成框架，支持可扩展、自适应且上下文感知的视频理解。代码将发布于https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/COPRA。

</details>


### [5] [Multimodal Object Detection Under Sparse Forest-Canopy Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15326)
*Nitik Jain,Mangal Kothari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种结合LiDAR、可见光-热成像融合和机载光学切片（AOS）的多模态探测管道，用于在林地冠层下检测人类目标。实验表明，LiDAR穿透植被能力有限，而可见光-热融合与AOS可分别提升低对比度场景和合成森林图像中的目标可见性，微调后的YOLOv5在FLIR数据集上达到约0.83的mAP。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在森林冠层下可靠检测人类目标仍然是一项困难的遥感挑战，主要由于稀疏、结构化且视点依赖的遮挡。现有方法在复杂森林环境中表现有限，亟需多模态融合与智能检测方法的探索。

Method: 论文构建了一个多模态概念验证管道，包含三个互补方法：(i) 实验评估LiDAR回波穿透植被的能力；(ii) 采用多尺度变换与稀疏表示框架进行可见光-热图像融合，增强人体显著性；(iii) 通过机载光学切片（AOS）合成孔径成像抑制冠层杂波。同时，在Teledyne FLIR热成像数据集上微调YOLOv5检测器，并在热成像和融合图像上进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，所测试的地基LiDAR配置在目标级检测上的穿透能力有限；可见光-热融合能改善低对比度场景中的目标可见性；AOS可增强合成森林图像中的地平面检测效果。微调后的YOLOv5在FLIR数据集的前三个类别上平均精度（mAP）约为0.83。

Conclusion: 这些发现为部署在森林环境中的无人机搜索救援与监视系统建立了初始基线，并激励未来在专用森林数据集和实时多模态集成方面的研究工作。

Original Abstract: Reliable detection of humans beneath forest canopy remains a difficult remote-sensing challenge due to sparse, structured, and viewpoint-dependent occlusion. This paper presents a multimodal proof-of-concept pipeline that integrates three complementary approaches: (i) experimental evaluation of LiDAR returns through vegetation to assess the feasibility of active sensing, (ii) visible--thermal image fusion using a multi-scale transform and sparse-representation framework to enhance human saliency, and (iii) synthetic-aperture image formation via Airborne Optical Sectioning (AOS) to suppress canopy clutter. A YOLOv5 detector is fine-tuned on the Teledyne FLIR thermal dataset and evaluated on thermal and fused imagery. Results show that the tested terrestrial LiDAR configuration provides limited penetration for object-level detection, while visible--thermal fusion improves target visibility in low-contrast scenes and AOS enhances ground-plane detection in synthetic forest imagery. The fine-tuned YOLOv5 achieves a mean average precision of $\sim$0.83 on the top three FLIR classes. These findings establish an initial baseline for UAV-deployable search-and-rescue and surveillance systems operating in forested environments, and motivate future work on dedicated forest datasets and real-time multimodal integration.

中文摘要翻译: 由于稀疏、结构化且依赖于视点的遮挡，在森林冠层下可靠地检测人类仍然是一项困难的遥感挑战。本文提出了一种多模态概念验证管道，整合了三种互补方法：(i) 实验评估LiDAR回波穿透植被的能力，以评估主动传感的可行性；(ii) 使用多尺度变换和稀疏表示框架进行可见光-热图像融合，以增强人体显著性；(iii) 通过机载光学切片（AOS）合成孔径成像，以抑制冠层杂波。在Teledyne FLIR热成像数据集上微调了YOLOv5检测器，并在热成像和融合图像上进行评估。结果表明，所测试的地基LiDAR配置在目标级检测上穿透能力有限，而可见光-热融合可改善低对比度场景中的目标可见性，AOS可增强合成森林图像中的地平面检测效果。微调后的YOLOv5在FLIR数据集的前三个类别上平均精度（mAP）约为0.83。这些发现为部署在森林环境中的无人机搜索救援与监视系统建立了初始基线，并激励未来在专用森林数据集和实时多模态集成方面的研究工作。

</details>


### [6] [Minerva-Ego: Spatiotemporal Hints for Egocentric Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15342)
*Arsha Nagrani,Jasper Uijilings,Shyamal Buch,Tobias Weyand,Sudheendra Vijayanarasimhan,Bo Hu,Ramin Mehran,David A Ross,Cordelia Schmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Minerva-Ego是一个用于评估复杂自我中心视觉推理的基准测试，包含多步多模态问题和密集的人工标注推理轨迹。实验表明最先进模型与人类表现仍有较大差距，而通过提示模型“在哪里”和“何时”看可以显著提升性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频推理基准仅评估最终输出（如问题答案），缺乏对中间推理步骤的评估，且大多局限于文本域，无法全面衡量模型在自我中心/具身场景下的复杂视觉推理能力。

Method: 基于近期高质量自我中心/具身视频数据源，扩展构建了包含多步多模态问题和时空密集人工标注推理轨迹的Minerva-Ego基准。每条推理轨迹还标注了解决问题所需的目标对象的时空掩码。

Result: 最先进模型与人类表现之间存在较大差距；通过向模型提供关于“在哪里”和“何时”看的提示，可以显著提升推理性能。

Conclusion: Minerva-Ego基准揭示了当前模型在复杂自我中心视觉推理中的不足，并指出时空定位提示是弥合模型与人类差距的有效方向。

Original Abstract: Video reasoning models are a core component of egocentric and embodied agents. However, standard benchmarks for assessing models provide only evaluation of the output (e.g. the answer to a question), without evaluation of intermediate reasoning steps, and most provide answers only in the text domain. We introduce Minerva-Ego, a benchmark for evaluating complex egocentric visual reasoning. We extend recent high-quality video data sources recorded from egocentric / embodied settings with a set of challenging, multi-step multimodal questions and spatiotemporally-dense human-annotated reasoning traces. Benchmarking experiments show that state-of-the-art models still have a large gap to human performance. To investigate this gap in detail, we annotate each reasoning trace in the dataset with the objects of interest required to solve the question, as spatiotemporal mask annotations. Through extensive evaluations, we identify that prompting frontier models with hints of 'where' and 'when' to look yields substantial improvements in performance. Minerva-Ego can be downloaded at https://github.com/google-deepmind/neptune.

中文摘要翻译: 视频推理模型是自我中心和具身智能体的核心组成部分。然而，用于评估模型的标准基准仅对输出（例如问题的答案）进行评估，而不评估中间推理步骤，并且大多数基准仅在文本领域提供答案。我们引入了Minerva-Ego，一个用于评估复杂自我中心视觉推理的基准。我们扩展了近期从自我中心/具身设置中记录的高质量视频数据源，为其配备了一套具有挑战性的多步多模态问题以及时空密集的人工标注推理轨迹。基准测试实验表明，最先进的模型与人类表现之间仍存在巨大差距。为了详细研究这一差距，我们对数据集中每条推理轨迹标注了解答问题所需的目标对象的时空掩码注释。通过广泛的评估，我们发现向前沿模型提示“在哪里”和“何时”看能带来性能的显著提升。Minerva-Ego可在https://github.com/google-deepmind/neptune下载。

</details>


### [7] [MR2-ByteTrack: CNN and Transformer-based Video Object Detection for AI-augmented Embedded Vision Sensor Nodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15423)
*Luca Bompani,Manuele Rusci,Luca Benini,Daniele Palossi,Francesco Conti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MR2-ByteTrack，一种面向MCU超低功耗嵌入式视觉节点的视频目标检测方法，通过交替全分辨率和低分辨率推理、帧间检测关联及重打分校正，在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算与能耗，首次在MCU上实现基于Transformer的实时视频目标检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代智能视觉传感器需要在设备端处理视频流，但云计算的带宽、延迟和隐私问题使其不可行。然而，这些传感系统通常依赖超低功耗MCU，其内存和算力极为有限，传统视频目标检测方法需要存储特征或多帧缓冲，无法在MCU上部署。

Method: MR2-ByteTrack采用多分辨率推理策略，交替处理全分辨率和低分辨率帧以降低计算开销；通过ByteTrack算法跨帧关联检测结果；引入Rescore算法，利用概率并集规则聚合跨帧的检测置信度分数，纠正误分类。

Result: 在ImageNetVID上，CNN模型mAP达49.0，Transformer模型达48.7；CNN乘加操作减少53%，Transformer减少32%；在GAP9 RISC-V多核MCU上部署，相比全分辨率处理节省最高55%能耗，首次在MCU上实现基于Transformer的实时视频目标检测。

Conclusion: MR2-ByteTrack在保持精度的同时大幅降低计算与能耗，具有跨架构通用性，为超低功耗嵌入式视觉节点上的实时视频目标检测提供可行方案。

Original Abstract: Modern smart vision sensors need on-device intelligence to process video streams, as cloud computing is often impractical due to bandwidth, latency, and privacy constraints. However, these sensory systems typically rely on ultra-low-power microcontrollers (MCUs) with limited memory and compute, making conventional video object detection methods, which require feature storage or multi-frame buffering, unfeasible. To address this challenge, we introduce Multi-Resolution Rescored ByteTrack (MR2-ByteTrack), a Video Object Detection (VOD) method tailored for MCU-based embedded vision nodes. MR2-ByteTrack reduces computational cost by alternating between full- and low-resolution inference, while linking detections across frames via ByteTrack and correcting misclassifications through the Rescore algorithm, which applies probability union rules to aggregate detection confidence scores across frames. We apply our approach to both a CNN-based detector and a Transformer-based model, demonstrating its generality across architectures with fundamentally different spatial processing. Experiments on ImageNetVID demonstrate that MR2-ByteTrack maintains accuracy, achieving mAP scores of up to 49.0 for the CNN-based models and 48.7 for the Transformer, while reducing multiply-accumulate operations by as much as 53\% for the CNNs and 32\% for the Transformer. When deployed on GAP9, an ultra-low-power RISC-V multicore MCU, our method yields up to 55\% energy savings compared to processing only full-resolution images, enabling the first real-time Transformer-based VOD on an MCU-class embedded vision node. Code available at https://github.com/Bomps4/Multi_Resolution_Rescored_ByteTrack/tree/IEEE_Access

中文摘要翻译: 现代智能视觉传感器需要在设备端具备处理视频流的智能能力，因为云计算常常因带宽、延迟和隐私约束而不切实际。然而，这些传感系统通常依赖具有有限内存和算力的超低功耗微控制器（MCU），这使得需要特征存储或多帧缓冲的传统视频目标检测方法不可行。为解决这一挑战，我们提出多分辨率重打分ByteTrack（MR2-ByteTrack），一种面向基于MCU的嵌入式视觉节点的视频目标检测（VOD）方法。MR2-ByteTrack通过在全分辨率和低分辨率推理之间交替进行来降低计算成本，同时通过ByteTrack跨帧关联检测结果，并利用重打分（Rescore）算法（该算法应用概率并集规则聚合跨帧的检测置信度分数）纠正误分类。我们将该方法应用于基于CNN的检测器和基于Transformer的模型，证明了其在不同空间处理方式的架构上的通用性。在ImageNetVID上的实验表明，MR2-ByteTrack在保持精度的同时，基于CNN的模型mAP达49.0，Transformer达48.7，同时CNN的乘加运算量减少高达53%，Transformer减少32%。当部署在GAP9（一款超低功耗RISC-V多核MCU）上时，与仅处理全分辨率图像相比，我们的方法节省高达55%的能耗，首次在MCU级嵌入式视觉节点上实现了基于Transformer的实时视频目标检测。代码已开源：https://github.com/Bomps4/Multi_Resolution_Rescored_ByteTrack/tree/IEEE_Access

</details>


### [8] [ChangeFlow -- Latent Rectified Flow for Change Detection in Remote Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15375)
*Blaž Rolih,Matic Fučka,Filip Wolf,Luka Čehovin Zajc*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ChangeFlow 提出了一种基于潜空间整流流（rectified flow）的生成式遥感变化检测框架，通过随机采样和集成预测提升鲁棒性与置信度估计，在四个基准上平均 F1 达 80.4%，超越此前最优方法 1.3 个点且推理速度与强判别基线相当。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有遥感变化检测方法要么采用逐像素判别式分类，无法将变化区域建模为连贯整体且缺乏对歧义性的处理；要么采用生成式方法但计算成本高、条件机制复杂，性能落后于判别式基线。本文旨在设计一种高效生成式框架，同时实现全局一致性、歧义建模和快速推理。

Method: ChangeFlow 将变化检测重新定义为潜空间中的变化掩码合成问题，使用整流流（rectified flow）进行生成建模；设计了一种结构化且轻量的条件信号引导生成过程；通过随机采样支持基于采样的预测集成，即聚合多个预测掩码提升鲁棒性，并利用样本一致性进行置信度估计以突出歧义区域。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，ChangeFlow 平均 F1 达到 80.4%，相比此前最优方法平均提升 1.3 个百分点，同时推理速度与近期强判别基线相当。

Conclusion: ChangeFlow 证明了生成式框架在遥感变化检测中的有效性，通过潜空间整流流实现了高效的生成建模，兼顾了性能、鲁棒性和推理效率，为变化检测任务提供了一种新的生成式方案。

Original Abstract: Remote sensing change detection (RSCD) aims to localise changes between two images of the same geographic region. In practice, change masks often follow region-level annotation conventions rather than purely local appearance differences, making them context-dependent and occasionally ambiguous. Most state-of-the-art methods utilise per-pixel discriminative classification, which produces a single prediction per input and fails to explicitly model the changed region as a coherent whole. A natural alternative is generative formulation, which can model a distribution of plausible masks, enabling sampling to capture ambiguity and encourage global consistency. However, existing generative RSCD approaches typically lag behind strong discriminative baselines due to the high computational cost of pixel-space generation and the complexity of their conditioning mechanisms. To address the limitations of prior discriminative and generative methods, we propose ChangeFlow, a generative framework that reformulates change detection as the synthesis of a change mask in latent space via rectified flow. ChangeFlow is guided by a structured yet lightweight conditioning signal, and its stochastic design naturally supports sampling-based prediction ensembling. Namely, aggregating multiple predicted change masks improves robustness, while sample agreement provides a practical confidence estimation that highlights ambiguous regions. Across four benchmarks, ChangeFlow achieves an average F1 of 80.4\%, improving by 1.3 points on average over the previous best method, while maintaining inference speed comparable to recent strong baselines. Project page: https://blaz-r.github.io/changeflow_cd

中文摘要翻译: 遥感变化检测（RSCD）旨在定位同一地理区域的两幅图像之间的变化。在实践中，变化掩码通常遵循区域级标注惯例而非纯粹的局部外观差异，因此具有上下文依赖性和偶尔的歧义性。大多数最先进的方法采用逐像素判别式分类，这为每个输入生成单一预测，无法将变化区域显式建模为一个连贯整体。一种自然的替代方案是生成式公式，它可以建模合理掩码的分布，通过采样捕获歧义性并鼓励全局一致性。然而，现有的生成式RSCD方法通常落后于强判别式基线，原因是像素空间生成的高计算成本及其条件机制的复杂性。为了解决先前判别式和生成式方法的局限性，我们提出了ChangeFlow，一种生成式框架，通过整流流（rectified flow）将变化检测重新定义为潜空间中变化掩码的合成。ChangeFlow由结构化且轻量的条件信号引导，其随机设计自然地支持基于采样的预测集成。具体来说，聚合多个预测的变化掩码提升了鲁棒性，而样本一致性提供了实用的置信度估计，突出显示了歧义区域。在四个基准上，ChangeFlow实现了平均F1为80.4%，相比此前最优方法平均提升1.3个点，同时保持了与近期强基线相当的推理速度。项目页面：https://blaz-r.github.io/changeflow_cd

</details>


### [9] [MorphoHELM: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating Representations for Microscopy-Based Morphology Assays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15383)
*Emre Hayir,Lorin Crawford,Alex X. Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphoHELM是一个用于评估细胞绘画（Cell Painting）特征提取方法的综合性开放基准，通过在不同程度的批次效应下评估各任务，揭示了现有方法在不同生物信号类型间的权衡，并发现经典计算机视觉分析策略在通用场景下仍优于所有深度学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 显微镜图像在药物筛选等应用中至关重要，但现有特征提取方法的评估标准分散且不统一，每个模型使用不同的任务、数据集、流水线和指标进行评估，导致难以公平比较不同方法。因此，需要一个统一的、全面的基准来标准化评估流程。

Method: MorphoHELM整合并扩展了该领域的评估标准，使其更加稳健；其核心特点是每个任务在不同程度的批次效应（技术噪声）下进行评估，直接量化方法检测生物信号的能力随噪声增加而衰减的程度，从而揭示方法间的权衡关系。

Result: 现有方法中没有任何模型在所有设置下优于经典的计算机视觉分析策略，这些经典方法仍然是通用场景下最强的表示。不同方法在不同类型的生物信号检测上表现出权衡，擅长某些生物信号的方法在其他信号上表现较弱。

Conclusion: MorphoHELM作为全面开放的基准，能够揭示不同特征提取方法之间的权衡特性，并表明经典计算机视觉策略仍是通用场景的最佳选择。该基准提供了公开的数据集、代码和评估工具，有助于推动该领域的标准化和公平比较。

Original Abstract: Microscopy images contain rich information about how cells respond to perturbations, making them essential to applications like drug screening. To quantify images, researchers often use representation extraction methods, and recent years have seen a proliferation of deep learning methods. While measuring the quality of these representations is essential, evaluation remains fragmented, with each proposed model evaluated on different tasks and datasets, using custom pipelines and metrics, making it difficult to fairly compare models. Here, we introduce MorphoHELM, a comprehensive open benchmark for evaluating feature extraction methods for Cell Painting, the most widely-used morphological profiling assay. MorphoHELM consolidates evaluation standards in the field, extends and corrects them to be more robust, and evaluates on the widest range of methods to date. A defining feature of the benchmark is that each task is evaluated at different degrees of batch effects (or technical noise), directly quantifying how the ability of methods to detect biological signal degrades as noise increases. Together, these properties enable MorphoHELM to detect trade-offs between methods, and we demonstrate that models that excel at certain kinds of biological signal are weaker at others. We show that no existing model outperforms classic computer vision analytic strategies across all settings, which remain the strongest general use-case representations. All datasets, code, and evaluation tools are publicly available at https://github.com/microsoft/MorphoHELM.

中文摘要翻译: 显微镜图像包含有关细胞如何响应扰动的丰富信息，使其在药物筛选等应用中至关重要。为了量化图像，研究人员通常使用表示提取方法，近年来深度学习方法大量涌现。虽然衡量这些表示的质量至关重要，但评估仍然分散，每个提出的模型都在不同的任务和数据集上，使用自定义流水线和指标进行评估，这使得公平比较模型变得困难。在此，我们介绍了MorphoHELM，一个用于评估细胞绘画（Cell Painting）——最广泛使用的形态学分析技术——特征提取方法的综合性开放基准。MorphoHELM整合了该领域的评估标准，扩展并修正这些标准使其更加稳健，并评估了迄今为止最广泛的方法。该基准的一个核心特点是每个任务都在不同程度的批次效应（或技术噪声）下进行评估，直接量化了方法检测生物信号的能力随噪声增加而衰减的程度。这些特性共同使MorphoHELM能够检测方法之间的权衡，我们证明了在某些生物信号上表现优异的模型在其他信号上则较弱。我们发现，在所有设置下，没有现有模型优于经典的计算机视觉分析策略，这些经典方法仍然是通用场景下最强的表示。所有数据集、代码和评估工具均公开提供，网址为https://github.com/microsoft/MorphoHELM。

</details>


### [10] [PanoWorld: Geometry-Consistent Panoramic Video World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15391)
*Le Jiang,Xiangyu Bai,Bishoy Galoaa,Shayda Moezzi,Caleb James Lee,Tooba Imtiaz,Edmund Yeh,Jennifer Dy,Yanzhi Wang,Sarah Ostadabbas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoWorld 是一种从单张图像和文字描述生成几何一致的全景视频的世界模型，通过引入深度一致性和轨迹一致性正则化，将全景视频生成视为几何与动力学一致的潜在状态建模问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全景视频方法仅优化视觉真实性，不约束底层3D场景状态，导致生成结果虽然看似合理但存在深度不一致、对应关系断裂以及球面上的运动不真实等问题。

Method: 在预训练的透视视频世界模型基础上，引入两种轻量级正则化器：深度一致性损失（基于伪真值全景深度）和轨迹一致性损失（监督跟踪点的3D世界帧位置），并应用球面几何感知的适应机制调整条件编码和位置编码。

Result: 实验表明PanoWorld在几何一致性上优于先前全景生成方法，同时保持有竞争力的视觉真实性。

Conclusion: 全景视频生成必须被视为几何建模问题，才能支持具身AI应用所需的整体空间理解能力。

Original Abstract: We present PanoWorld, a panoramic video world model that generates geometry-consistent 360$\degree$ video from a single image and a caption. Existing panoramic video methods optimize primarily for visual realism and do not explicitly constrain the underlying 3D scene state, producing outputs that appear plausible yet exhibit inconsistent depth, broken correspondences, and implausible motion across the spherical surface. We address this gap by framing panoramic video generation as a geometry- and dynamics-consistent latent state modeling problem rather than pure visual synthesis. Building on a pre-trained perspective video world model, we introduce two lightweight regularizers: a depth consistency loss against pseudo ground-truth panoramic depth, and a trajectory consistency loss that supervises the 3D world-frame positions of tracked points across time. We further apply spherical-geometry-aware adaptation to the conditioning and positional encoding. We additionally introduce PanoGeo, a unified geometry-aware panoramic video dataset with consistent depth, trajectory, and prompt annotations across diverse real and synthetic sources, used for both training and stratified evaluation. Experiments show that PanoWorld improves geometric consistency over prior panoramic generation methods while maintaining competitive visual realism, establishing that panoramic video generation must be treated as a geometric modeling problem to support the holistic spatial understanding requirements of embodied AI applications. Code is available at https://github.com/ostadabbas/PanoWorld.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了PanoWorld，一个全景视频世界模型，能够从单张图像和文字描述生成几何一致的360°视频。现有的全景视频方法主要优化视觉真实性，而未显式约束底层3D场景状态，因此产生的输出虽然看似合理，但存在深度不一致、对应关系断裂以及球面上运动不真实的问题。我们通过将全景视频生成视为一个几何与动力学一致的潜在状态建模问题（而非纯粹的视觉合成问题）来填补这一空白。在预训练的透视视频世界模型的基础上，我们引入了两种轻量级正则化器：针对伪真值全景深度的深度一致性损失，以及监督跟踪点随时间变化的3D世界帧位置的轨迹一致性损失。我们还对条件编码和位置编码应用了球面几何感知的适应机制。此外，我们引入了PanoGeo，一个统一的全景视频几何感知数据集，包含来自多种真实和合成数据源的深度、轨迹和提示标注，用于训练和分层评估。实验表明，PanoWorld在几何一致性上优于先前全景生成方法，同时保持有竞争力的视觉真实性，从而确立了全景视频生成必须被视为几何建模问题，以支持具身AI应用所需的整体空间理解能力。代码已开源：https://github.com/ostadabbas/PanoWorld。

</details>


### [11] [ELDOR: A Dataset and Benchmark for Illegal Gold Mining in the Amazon Rainforest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15397)
*Kangning Cui,Surendra Bohara,Suraj Prasai,Zishan Shao,Wei Tang,Martin Pillaca,Edwin Flores,Zhen Yang,Gregory Larsen,Evan Dethier,David Lutz,Jean-Michel Morel,Miles Silman,Victor Pauca,Fan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ELDOR——一个大规模无人机基准数据集，用于监测亚马逊雨林中非法金矿开采造成的环境和景观破坏。该数据集包含超过2500公顷的手动标注正射影像，涵盖语义分割、多标签分类等四个基准任务。实验表明现有方法在检测小型采矿结构和细粒度恢复类别方面仍存在困难，需要上下文感知和多模态建模。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 亚马逊雨林非法金矿开采导致森林砍伐、水污染和生态系统破坏，但卫星影像难以监测小尺度采矿结构和细微土地覆盖变化，尤其在频繁云覆盖条件下。现有遥感监测方法在细粒度识别方面存在局限，因此需要高分辨率、精细标注的无人机基准数据集来推动相关研究。

Method: 构建了ELDOR数据集，包含覆盖2500多公顷的无人机正射影像，提供像素级语义标注。在此基础上建立了四个基准任务：(1)语义分割、(2)基于分割的识别、(3)直接多标签分类、(4)基于视觉语言模型的类别存在识别。对比了通用和遥感专用的分割模型、视觉基础模型相关的分割方法、直接多标签分类方法以及视觉语言模型。

Result: 现有方法在处理稀有小型采矿结构和细粒度恢复类别时仍然表现不佳，表明需要上下文感知和多模态建模方法。同时构建了交互式探索工具，为领域专家提供统一的数据探索和模型推理界面。

Conclusion: 当前方法在监测非法金矿开采导致的微小环境变化方面仍存在明显不足，需要发展上下文感知和多模态模型。ELDOR数据集和基准任务为这一方向提供了重要的评估平台。

Original Abstract: Illegal gold mining in the Amazon rainforest causes deforestation, water contamination, and long-term ecosystem disruption, yet remains difficult to monitor at fine spatial scales. Satellite imagery supports large-scale observation, but often misses small mining-related structures and subtle land-cover transitions, especially under frequent cloud cover. We introduce ELDOR, a large-scale UAV benchmark for monitoring environmental and landscape disturbance from illegal gold mining in the rainforest. ELDOR contains manually annotated orthomosaic imagery covering over 2,500 hectares, with pixel-level semantic labels for both mining-related activities and surrounding ecological structures. With this unified annotation source, we establish four benchmark tasks: semantic segmentation, segmentation-derived recognition, direct multi-label classification, and class-presence recognition with vision-language models. Across these tasks, we compare generic and remote-sensing-specific segmentation models, vision foundation model-related segmentation methods, direct multi-label classification methods, and vision-language models under a controlled closed-set protocol. Results show that current methods still struggle with rare small-scale mining structures and fine-grained recovery classes, suggesting the need for context-aware and multimodal modeling. To support domain analysis and practical use, we further build an interactive explorer for domain experts that provides a unified interface for data exploration and model inference.

中文摘要翻译: 亚马逊雨林中的非法金矿开采导致森林砍伐、水体污染以及长期生态系统破坏，然而在精细空间尺度上对其进行监测仍然困难。卫星影像支持大规模观测，但常常遗漏小型采矿相关结构和细微的土地覆盖变化，尤其在频繁云覆盖条件下。本文提出了ELDOR——一个用于监测雨林中非法金矿开采所导致的环境和景观破坏的大规模无人机基准数据集。ELDOR包含覆盖超过2500公顷的手动标注正射影像，提供采矿相关活动及周围生态结构的像素级语义标签。借助这一统一标注源，我们建立了四个基准任务：语义分割、基于分割的识别、直接多标签分类，以及基于视觉语言模型的类别存在识别。在这些任务中，我们比较了通用与遥感专用分割模型、视觉基础模型相关的分割方法、直接多标签分类方法，以及受控闭集协议下的视觉语言模型。结果表明，当前方法在处理稀有小型采矿结构和细粒度恢复类别时仍然困难，表明需要上下文感知和多模态建模。为支持领域分析和实际应用，我们进一步为领域专家构建了一个交互式探索工具，提供统一的数据探索和模型推理界面。

</details>


### [12] [Social-Mamba: Socially-Aware Trajectory Forecasting with State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15424)
*Po-Chien Luan,Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Social-Mamba提出了一种基于选择性状态空间模型的轨迹预测架构，通过Cycle Mamba模块和社交三元组分解，在保持线性计算复杂度的同时实现了最先进的预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力机制的轨迹预测方法虽然能捕捉复杂社交依赖关系，但二次计算复杂度在密集人群中扩展性差；而选择性状态空间模型虽为线性时间替代方案，但其固有顺序设计与社会互动的非结构化和动态特性不匹配。

Method: 提出Social-Mamba架构，核心是Cycle Mamba模块实现连续双向信息流；通过自我中心网格组织智能体，引入社交三元组分解将交互分解为时间、自我中心和目标中心扫描，并通过可学习的社交门控和全局扫描动态整合这些信息。

Result: 在五个轨迹预测基准上取得最先进精度，同时具有更优的参数效率和计算可扩展性。将Social-Mamba嵌入流匹配框架后进一步提升了准确性和效率。

Conclusion: Social-Mamba为轨迹预测提供了一种灵活且鲁棒的线性时间替代方案，平衡了精度与计算效率，可作为未来轨迹预测研究的基础框架。

Original Abstract: Human trajectory forecasting is crucial for safe navigation in crowded environments, requiring models that balance accuracy with computational efficiency. Efficiently modeling social interactions is key to performance in dense crowds. Yet, most recent methods rely on attention mechanisms, which are effective at capturing complex dependencies, but incur quadratic computational costs that scale poorly with the growing number of neighbors. Recently, Selective State-Space Models have provided a linear-time alternative; however, their inherently sequential design is misaligned with the unstructured and dynamic nature of social interactions. To address this challenge, we propose Social-Mamba, a forecasting architecture that reformulates social interactions as structured sequential processes. At its core is the Cycle Mamba block, a novel module that enables continuous bidirectional information flow. Social-Mamba organizes agents on an egocentric grid and introduces social triplet factorization, which decomposes interactions into temporal, egocentric, and goal-centric scans. These are dynamically integrated through a learnable social gate and global scan to generate accurate and efficient trajectory predictions. Extensive experiments on five trajectory forecasting benchmarks show that Social-Mamba achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while offering superior parameter efficiency and computational scalability. Furthermore, embedding Social-Mamba into a flow-matching framework further enhances both accuracy and efficiency, establishing it as a flexible and robust foundation for future trajectory forecasting research. The code is publicly available: https://github.com/vita-epfl/Social-Mamba

中文摘要翻译: 人体轨迹预测对于拥挤环境中的安全导航至关重要，需要模型在准确性与计算效率之间取得平衡。高效建模社交互动是密集人群场景下性能的关键。然而，大多数近期方法依赖注意力机制，其虽能有效捕捉复杂依赖关系，但二次计算成本随邻居数量增长而扩展性差。最近，选择性状态空间模型提供了线性时间替代方案；然而，其固有的顺序设计与社交互动的非结构化和动态特性不匹配。为应对这一挑战，我们提出Social-Mamba，一种将社交互动重构为结构化序列过程的预测架构。其核心是Cycle Mamba模块，一种支持连续双向信息流的新型模块。Social-Mamba将智能体组织在自我中心网格上，并引入社交三元组分解，将交互分解为时间扫描、自我中心扫描和目标中心扫描。这些扫描通过可学习的社交门控和全局扫描动态整合，生成准确高效的轨迹预测。在五个轨迹预测基准上的大量实验表明，Social-Mamba在实现最先进精度的同时，提供了优越的参数效率和计算可扩展性。此外，将Social-Mamba嵌入流匹配框架进一步提升了准确性和效率，使其成为未来轨迹预测研究的灵活且鲁棒的基础框架。代码已公开：https://github.com/vita-epfl/Social-Mamba

</details>


### [13] [RIDE: Retinex-Informed Decoupling for Exposing Concealed Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15450)
*Chunming He,Rihan Zhang,Dingming Zhang,Chengyu Fang,Longxiang Tang,Jingjia Feng,Fengyang Xiao,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Retinex理论的同质图像分解方法RIDE，通过将图像分解为光照和反射分量来增强隐蔽目标分割的判别性，并理论证明了该分解在物理机制上总能保持或提升前景-背景的可区分性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有隐蔽目标分割方法要么直接操作RGB图像，要么采用傅里叶、小波等异质分解，这些分解会重新分布空间证据，导致逐像素对齐线索不够直接。作者发现视觉伪装在复合空间中强制外观匹配，但并不意味着在两个分量空间中也必须同时匹配，这启发他们利用Retinex分解来打破这种匹配。

Method: (i) 任务驱动的Retinex分解模块：端到端学习分割最优的分解；(ii) 判别性差距注意力机制：自适应地利用分解能带来帮助的区域；(iii) 伪装破除对比损失：在反射特征空间中施加对比学习。

Result: 理论证明了判别性差距定理：不同COS子任务中，物理过程系统地反相关光照和反射差异，Retinex分解在整个物理范围内总能保持或严格提升前景-背景的总可区分性，反相关性最大化增益。

Conclusion: 通过Retinex同质分解，隐蔽目标分割的性能可以得到理论保证的提升，该方法统一了多种伪装场景的底层物理机制，提供了新的解决范式。

Original Abstract: Concealed Object Segmentation (COS) encompasses a family of dense-prediction tasks, including camouflaged object detection, polyp segmentation, transparent object detection, and industrial defect inspection, where targets are visually entangled with their surroundings through different physical mechanisms. Existing methods either operate directly on RGB images or employ \emph{heterogeneous} decompositions (\eg, Fourier, wavelet) that redistribute spatial evidence across scale/frequency coefficients, making pixel-aligned cues less direct. We introduce a fundamentally different perspective: \textbf{homogeneous image decomposition} via Retinex theory, which factorizes an image into illumination and reflectance components within the \emph{same} spatial domain. Our key insight is that visual entanglement enforces appearance matching in the composite space, but this does \emph{not} necessitate simultaneous matching in both component spaces, a phenomenon we formalize as the \textbf{Discriminability Gap Theorem}. Crucially, we show that across diverse COS sub-tasks, the underlying physical processes systematically anti-correlate illumination and reflectance differences, yielding theoretical guarantees that Retinex decomposition preserves or strictly improves total foreground--background discriminability across the full physical regime, with anti-correlation maximizing the gain. Building on this, we propose \textbf{RIDE} comprising: (i) a Task-Driven Retinex Decomposition module that learns segmentation-optimal factorizations end-to-end; (ii) a Discriminability Gap Attention mechanism that adaptively exploits where decomposition helps; and (iii) a Camouflage-Breaking Contrastive loss operating in reflectance feature space.

中文摘要翻译: 隐蔽目标分割（COS）涵盖了一系列密集预测任务，包括伪装目标检测、息肉分割、透明目标检测和工业缺陷检测，在这些任务中，目标通过不同的物理机制与周围环境在视觉上纠缠在一起。现有方法要么直接在RGB图像上操作，要么采用异质分解（如傅里叶变换、小波变换），这些分解将空间证据重新分布到尺度/频率系数中，使得逐像素对齐的线索变得不够直接。我们引入了一个根本不同的视角：基于Retinex理论的同质图像分解，该理论将图像分解为同一空间域内的光照分量和反射分量。我们的关键洞察在于，视觉纠缠在合成空间强制了外观匹配，但这并不要求在两个分量空间中同时进行匹配——我们将这一现象形式化为判别性差距定理。重要的是，我们证明了在不同COS子任务中，底层物理过程系统地反相关光照和反射差异，从而提供了理论保证：Retinex分解在完整的物理范围内总能保持或严格提升前景-背景的总可区分性，且反相关性最大化了增益。基于此，我们提出了RIDE，包括：(i) 任务驱动的Retinex分解模块，端到端学习分割最优的分解方式；(ii) 判别性差距注意力机制，自适应地利用分解能带来帮助的区域；(iii) 伪装破除对比损失，在反射特征空间中发挥作用。

</details>


### [14] [Video Models Can Reason with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15458)
*Tinghui Zhu,Sheng Zhang,James Y. Huang,Selena Song,Xiaofei Wen,Yuankai Li,Hoifung Poon,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VideoRLVR，一种将带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）应用于视频扩散模型的训练方法，通过SDE-GRPO优化、密集分解奖励和早期步骤聚焦策略，使模型在满足空间、时间和逻辑约束的推理任务上超越监督微调和现有视频生成模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在感知真实性和时间连贯性上进展迅速，但主要针对"合理生成"而非"可验证推理"进行优化，在需要满足显式空间、时间或逻辑约束的任务中表现不佳。受RLVR在推理导向语言模型中的成功启发，作者希望将类似范式引入视频生成领域。

Method: 提出VideoRLVR框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) SDE-GRPO优化骨干，将GRPO（带可验证奖励的群体相对策略优化）适配到视频扩散模型的SDE去噪过程中；(2) 密集分解奖励，在去噪轨迹的多个步骤提供细粒度奖励信号；(3) Early-Step Focus策略，仅在早期去噪阶段进行策略优化，减少约40%训练延迟同时保持性能。

Result: 在Maze、FlowFree和Sokoban三个程序化生成的推理基准上，VideoRLVR持续优于监督微调基线；密集分解奖励在低成功率场景中尤为重要；优化后的模型在可验证推理基准和域外基准上均超越了评估的闭源和开源视频生成模型。

Conclusion: 可验证的强化学习能够将视频模型从感知模仿推向更可靠的、符合规则的视觉推理，表明RLVR范式在视频生成领域具有重要潜力。

Original Abstract: Video diffusion models have made rapid progress in perceptual realism and temporal coherence, but they remain primarily optimized for plausible generation rather than verifiable reasoning. This limitation is especially pronounced in tasks where generated videos must satisfy explicit spatial, temporal, or logical constraints. Inspired by the role of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) in reasoning-oriented language models, we introduce VideoRLVR, a practical recipe for optimizing video diffusion models with rule-based feedback. VideoRLVR formulates video reasoning as the generation of verifiable visual trajectories and consists of an SDE-GRPO optimization backbone, dense decomposed rewards, and an Early-Step Focus strategy for efficient training. The Early-Step Focus strategy restricts policy optimization to the early denoising phase, reducing training latency by about 40% while preserving performance. We evaluate VideoRLVR on Maze, FlowFree, and Sokoban, three procedurally generated domains with objective success criteria. Across these tasks, VideoRLVR consistently improves over supervised fine-tuning baselines, with dense decomposed rewards proving especially important in low-success-rate settings. Our RL-optimized model also outperforms the evaluated proprietary and open-source video generation models on these verifiable reasoning benchmarks and out-of-domain benchmarks. These results suggest that verifiable RL can move video models beyond perceptual imitation toward more reliable rule-consistent visual reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 视频扩散模型在感知真实性和时间连贯性方面取得了快速进展，但它们仍然主要针对合理生成而非可验证推理进行优化。这一局限在生成视频必须满足显式空间、时间或逻辑约束的任务中尤为突出。受带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在推理导向语言模型中的作用的启发，我们提出了VideoRLVR——一种利用基于规则的反馈优化视频扩散模型的实用方案。VideoRLVR将视频推理形式化为可验证视觉轨迹的生成，由SDE-GRPO优化骨干、密集分解奖励以及用于高效训练的早期步骤聚焦策略（Early-Step Focus）组成。早期步骤聚焦策略将策略优化限制在早期去噪阶段，可在保持性能的同时减少约40%的训练延迟。我们在Maze、FlowFree和Sokoban这三个具有客观成功标准的程序化生成领域上评估了VideoRLVR。在这些任务中，VideoRLVR持续优于监督微调基线，其中密集分解奖励在低成功率设置中尤为重要。我们的RL优化模型在可验证推理基准和域外基准上也优于被评估的闭源和开源视频生成模型。这些结果表明，可验证的RL可以将视频模型从感知模仿推向更可靠的、符合规则的视觉推理。

</details>


### [15] [Entity-Centric World Models: Interaction-Aware Masking for Causal Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15466)
*Santosh Kumar Paidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Interaction-Aware JEPA (IA-JEPA)，通过自监督运动中心掩码策略优先处理物理交互，打破了标准自监督学习中的"静态偏差"，在因果推理任务上显著超越基线，并泛化到真实世界人类动作和零样本物理谜题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准JEPA架构在语义分类上表现优异，但采用基于补丁的掩码策略，优先关注视觉纹理而忽略稀有的运动学事件，导致无法捕捉因果动力学，即存在"物理盲"问题。本文旨在解决这一缺陷。

Method: 提出IA-JEPA，采用自监督运动中心掩码策略，专门针对参与碰撞或动量传递的实体进行掩码，迫使架构重建潜在轨迹而非静态背景特征，从而优先学习物理交互。

Result: 在CLEVRER基准上，因果推理任务准确率14.26%（基线仅3.22%）；潜在空间熵增10%，物理能量线性化R²=0.43；泛化到Something-Something V2和PHYRE-Lite数据集。

Conclusion: IA-JEPA提供了一条可扩展的、完全自监督的路径，用于构建能够内化物理世界因果结构的基础世界模型，打破了标准自监督的"静态偏差"。

Original Abstract: Learning predictive world models from unlabelled video is a foundational challenge in artificial intelligence. While Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA) have set new benchmarks in semantic classification, they often remain physics-blind, failing to capture the causal dynamics necessary for downstream reasoning. We hypothesize that this stems from standard patch-based masking strategies, which prioritize visual texture over rare but informative kinematic events. We propose Interaction-Aware JEPA (IA-JEPA), which utilizes a self-supervised motion-centric masking strategy to prioritize physical interactions. By specifically targeting entities engaged in collisions or momentum transfers, we force the architecture to reconstruct latent trajectories rather than static background features. Evaluated on the CLEVRER benchmark, IA-JEPA achieves 14.26% accuracy on causal reasoning tasks, a significant lead over the 3.22% achieved by standard patch-masked baselines. Crucially, we demonstrate that IA-JEPA breaks the "static bias" of standard self-supervision by inducing a higher-entropy, more discriminative latent space (+10% entropy gain) that linearizes physical energy ($R^2=0.43$). We show that this interaction bias generalizes to real-world human actions (Something-Something V2) and zero-shot physical puzzles (PHYRE-Lite). Our results provide a scalable, fully self-supervised path toward building foundational world models that begin to internalize the causal structure of the physical world.

中文摘要翻译: 从无标注视频中学习预测性世界模型是人工智能领域的一项基础性挑战。尽管联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在语义分类上树立了新的标杆，但它们往往存在"物理盲"问题，无法捕获下游推理所需的因果动力学。我们假设，这一问题源于标准的基于补丁的掩码策略，该策略优先关注视觉纹理而非稀有但具有信息量的运动学事件。我们提出交互感知JEPA（IA-JEPA），它采用一种自监督的以运动为中心的掩码策略，优先处理物理交互。通过专门针对参与碰撞或动量传递的实体进行掩码，我们迫使架构重建潜在轨迹而非静态背景特征。在CLEVRER基准上的评估表明，IA-JEPA在因果推理任务上达到了14.26%的准确率，远超标准补丁掩码基线所取得的3.22%。关键的是，我们证明IA-JEPA通过诱导一个更高熵、更具判别性的潜在空间（熵增益+10%），打破了标准自监督学习的"静态偏差"，并使物理能量线性化（R²=0.43）。我们展示了这种交互偏差能够泛化到真实世界的人类动作（Something-Something V2）和零样本物理谜题（PHYRE-Lite）。我们的结果为构建可扩展、完全自监督的基础世界模型提供了路径，这些模型开始内化物理世界的因果结构。

</details>


### [16] [EgoExo-WM: Unlocking Exo Video for Ego World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15477)
*Danny Tran,Roberto Martín-Martín,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过从外视角视频中提取结构化人体姿态并利用人类运动学先验将其转换为第一人称视角视频，从而利用大量外视角数据训练自我中心世界模型，显著提升预测质量和规划性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自我中心世界模型受限于第一人称训练数据的稀缺性和其对人体动作的部分可观测性，而外视角视频虽然丰富且能清晰展现身体姿态，但与智能体的动作空间缺乏直接对齐。本文旨在弥合这一差距，利用外视角视频赋能自我中心世界模型训练。

Method: 从外视角视频中提取结构化人体姿态作为动作表征，结合人类运动学先验，将外视角视频转换为自我中心视频，从而实现将任意野外外视角数据整合到自我中心世界模型的训练中。

Result: 训练基于全身动作条件的自我中心世界模型时，使用转换后的外视角数据显著提升了预测质量和下游规划性能（推断实现视觉目标状态所需的姿态序列）。

Conclusion: 该方法为利用任意野外视频构建强大的自我中心世界模型铺平了道路，推动了机器人规划和增强现实指导等应用的发展。

Original Abstract: Egocentric world models present a promising direction for enabling agents to predict and plan, but their performance is constrained by the limited availability of egocentric training data and its inherent partial observability of humans' physical actions. In contrast, exocentric video is abundant and reveals body poses well, but lacks direct alignment with an agent's action space -- and is not egocentric. We propose a method to bridge this gap by extracting structured body pose from exocentric video as a representation of action and transforming the exocentric video to egocentric video, informed by a human kinematics prior. This process unlocks the integration of in-the-wild exocentric data for egocentric world model training. We show that training whole-body action-conditioned egocentric world models with our converted data significantly improves both prediction quality and downstream planning performance, where we infer the sequence of body poses needed to achieve a visual goal state. Our approach paves the way to enlist arbitrary in-the-wild videos for building powerful egocentric world models, furthering applications in robot planning and augmented-reality guidance.

中文摘要翻译: 自我中心世界模型为智能体进行预测和规划提供了一个有前景的方向，但其性能受限于自我中心训练数据的匮乏以及该类数据对人类身体动作固有的部分可观测性。相比之下，外视角视频数据丰富且能清晰地展现身体姿态，但缺乏与智能体动作空间的直接对齐——并且它不是自我中心的。我们提出了一种弥合这一差距的方法：从外视角视频中提取结构化人体姿态作为动作表征，并借助人类运动学先验将外视角视频转换为自我中心视频。这一过程实现了将任意野外外视角数据整合到自我中心世界模型的训练中。我们证明，使用转换后的数据训练基于全身动作条件的自我中心世界模型，能够显著提升预测质量和下游规划性能——即推断出实现视觉目标状态所需的姿态序列。我们的方法为利用任意野外视频构建强大的自我中心世界模型铺平了道路，推动了机器人规划和增强现实指导等应用的发展。

</details>


### [17] [AnyAct: Towards Human Reenactment of Character Motion From Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15497)
*Liuhan Chen,Lei Zhong,Jiewei Wang,Qin Shuai,Li Yuan,Leidong Fan,Qing Li,Kanglin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnyAct，从非人类角色单目视频直接生成可编辑的人类重演动画，通过稀疏局部2D关节点运动作为跨结构桥梁。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于视频的动作捕捉方法局限于人体结构空间，动作重定向方法需要结构化3D源运动及源拓扑，无法直接从非人类角色视频生成人类重演。

Method: AnyAct将角色视频驱动的人类重演建模为基于可迁移稀疏局部2D关节点运动的条件人类运动生成。关键设计包括：通过增强3D到2D投影实现仅人类运动监督、渐进式3D到2D训练缓解条件歧义、全局-局部运动解耦实现可靠的局部运动控制。

Result: 在覆盖多种非人类角色视频的基准测试上，AnyAct生成高保真初始人类重演，保留参考视频中角色的本质动态；消融实验验证了核心设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 稀疏局部关节点运动可在较大结构差异下保留关键动态，为从角色视频到人类重演提供稳定桥梁。

Original Abstract: We study the problem of directly deriving an initial human reenactment from a monocular video of a non-human character. Our goal is not to reconstruct the source character itself but to reinterpret its motion as a plausible and editable human performance for downstream animation authoring. This task is challenging because existing video-based motion capture methods are largely restricted to human-centric structural spaces, while motion retargeting methods typically require structured 3D source motions and known source topologies. Our key insight is that sparse local articulated motion cues can preserve essential dynamics across large structural differences, providing a stable bridge from character video to human reenactment. Based on this observation, we propose AnyAct, which formulates character-video-driven human reenactment as conditional human motion generation from transferable sparse local 2D articulated motion. To make this practical, we introduce three key designs: human-motion-only supervision via augmented 3D-to-2D projection, progressive 3D-to-2D training to alleviate conditioning ambiguity, and global-local motion decoupling for reliable local motion control. We further construct a benchmark primarily covering diverse non-human character videos. Experiments on the benchmark show that AnyAct produces high-fidelity initial human reenactments that preserve the essential dynamics of the characters in reference videos, and further ablation studies validate the effectiveness of its core designs.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究从非人类角色的单目视频直接推导出初始人类重演的问题。我们的目标不是重建源角色本身，而是将其运动重新解释为合理且可编辑的人类表现，以支持下游动画创作。这一任务具有挑战性，因为现有的基于视频的动作捕捉方法主要局限于以人为中心的结构空间，而运动重定向方法通常需要结构化的3D源运动及已知的源拓扑。我们的关键洞察是，稀疏的局部关节点运动线索可以在较大的结构差异下保留本质动态，为从角色视频到人类重演提供稳定桥梁。基于这一观察，我们提出了AnyAct，它将角色视频驱动的人类重演表述为基于可迁移的稀疏局部2D关节点运动的条件人类运动生成。为使该方法实用化，我们引入了三项关键设计：通过增强3D到2D投影实现仅人类运动监督、渐进式3D到2D训练以缓解条件歧义、以及全局-局部运动解耦以实现可靠的局部运动控制。我们进一步构建了一个主要覆盖多种非人类角色视频的基准测试。在该基准上的实验表明，AnyAct能够生成高保真的初始人类重演，保留参考视频中角色的本质动态，进一步的消融研究验证了其核心设计的有效性。

</details>


### [18] [DiffVAS: Diffusion-Guided Visual Active Search in Partially Observable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15519)
*Anindya Sarkar,Srikumar Sastry,Aleksis Pirinen,Nathan Jacobs,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DiffVAS，一种利用扩散模型和强化学习的目标条件策略，用于在部分可观测环境中同时搜索多种目标，推进视觉主动搜索在现实场景中的应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VAS方法假设搜索空间完全已知（不现实）且只能搜索单一目标类别，无法在部分可观测环境中同时搜索多种目标。

Method: DiffVAS采用扩散模型从顺序观测的局部片段重建整个地理空间，再结合目标条件强化学习规划模块来推理并引导后续搜索步骤。

Result: 在多个数据集上，DiffVAS在部分可观测环境下搜索多样目标的表现显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DiffVAS通过扩散模型+强化学习的范式，有效解决了部分可观测环境中多目标同时搜索的难题，推进了VAS在真实世界部署的可行性。

Original Abstract: Visual active search (VAS) has been introduced as a modeling framework that leverages visual cues to direct aerial (e.g., UAV-based) exploration and pinpoint areas of interest within extensive geospatial regions. Potential applications of VAS include detecting hotspots for rare wildlife poaching, aiding search-and-rescue missions, and uncovering illegal trafficking of weapons, among other uses. Previous VAS approaches assume that the entire search space is known upfront, which is often unrealistic due to constraints such as a restricted field of view and high acquisition costs, and they typically learn policies tailored to specific target objects, which limits their ability to search for multiple target categories simultaneously. In this work, we propose DiffVAS, a target-conditioned policy that searches for diverse objects simultaneously according to task requirements in partially observable environments, which advances the deployment of visual active search policies in real-world applications. DiffVAS leverages a diffusion model to reconstruct the entire geospatial area from sequentially observed partial glimpses, which enables a target-conditioned reinforcement learning-based planning module to effectively reason and guide subsequent search steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DiffVAS excels in searching diverse objects in partially observable environments, significantly surpassing state-of-the-art methods on several datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉主动搜索（VAS）作为一种建模框架已被引入，它利用视觉线索引导基于飞行器（如无人机）的探索，并在广阔的地理空间区域内精确定位感兴趣区域。VAS的潜在应用包括检测稀有野生动物偷猎热点、辅助搜索救援任务以及发现非法武器贩运等。以往的VAS方法假设整个搜索空间是预先完全已知的，这通常因视野受限和高采集成本等约束而不切实际，并且它们通常学习针对特定目标对象的策略，这限制了同时搜索多个目标类别的能力。在本工作中，我们提出DiffVAS，一种目标条件策略，能够根据任务需求在部分可观测环境中同时搜索多样化的目标，这推进了视觉主动搜索策略在现实应用中的部署。DiffVAS利用扩散模型从顺序观测的局部片段重建整个地理空间区域，这使得基于目标条件强化学习的规划模块能够有效推理并引导后续搜索步骤。大量实验表明，DiffVAS在部分可观测环境中搜索多样化目标方面表现出色，在多个数据集上显著超越了现有最先进方法。

</details>


### [19] [Self-Prompting Diffusion Transformer for Open-Vocabulary Scene Text Editing via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15523)
*Hongxi Li,Tong Wang,Chengjing Wu,Tianbao Liu,Jiangtao Yao,Xiaochao Qu,Xinxiao Wu,Luoqi Liu,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自提示场景文本编辑方法（MSTEdit），利用多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）的上下文学习能力，直接从原始图像构建风格和字形提示，无需额外编码器，并通过两阶段训练实现开放词汇、风格一致的文本编辑，在多种语言上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有场景文本编辑方法仅依赖图像背景信息，忽略了目标区域的视觉细节，丢弃了原始文本的风格特征，实质退化为文本渲染任务；此外，预训练字形编码器限制了可编辑文本的范围。

Method: 提出自提示场景文本编辑方法，直接从原始图像构建风格和字形提示，不引入额外的风格或字形编码器；采用两阶段训练策略：先在大型自监督数据上训练扩散Transformer，再使用少量配对图像进行微调；利用多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）的上下文学习能力实现开放词汇和风格一致的文本编辑。

Result: 在多种语言上的实验结果表明，该方法在文本准确性和风格一致性方面均达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过自提示机制和MM-DiT的上下文学习能力，有效解决了现有方法忽略原始文本风格特征和编辑范围受限的问题，实现了开放词汇、风格一致的场景文本编辑。

Original Abstract: Scene text editing aims to modify text in a target region of an image while preserving surrounding background style and texture. Existing methods rely solely on image background information while neglecting the visual details of target regions, which discards stylistic features in the original text and essentially degrades the task to text rendering. Moreover, the conditions imposed by pre-trained glyph encoder limit the scope of editable text. To address these issues, this paper proposes a self-prompting scene text editing method that constructs style and glyph prompts directly from the original image, without introducing additional style or glyph encoders. We employ a two-stage training strategy: the diffusion transformer is first trained on large-scale self-supervised data and then refined using a small set of paired images. By leveraging the in-context learning capability of the Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformer (MM-DiT), it achieves open-vocabulary and style-consistent text editing. Experimental results on various languages demonstrate that our method achieves the state-of-the-art performance in both text accuracy and style consistency. Our project page: \href{https://hongxiii.github.io/mstedit}{hongxiii.github.io/mstedit}.

中文摘要翻译: 场景文本编辑旨在修改图像目标区域中的文本，同时保留周围的背景风格和纹理。现有方法仅依赖图像背景信息，忽略了目标区域的视觉细节，这丢弃了原始文本中的风格特征，实质上将任务退化为文本渲染。此外，预训练字形编码器施加的条件限制了可编辑文本的范围。为解决这些问题，本文提出一种自提示场景文本编辑方法，直接从原始图像构建风格和字形提示，无需引入额外的风格或字形编码器。我们采用两阶段训练策略：扩散Transformer首先在大规模自监督数据上进行训练，然后使用少量配对图像进行微调。通过利用多模态扩散Transformer（MM-DiT）的上下文学习能力，实现了开放词汇和风格一致的文本编辑。在多种语言上的实验结果表明，我们的方法在文本准确性和风格一致性方面均达到了最先进的性能。项目页面：\href{https://hongxiii.github.io/mstedit}{hongxiii.github.io/mstedit}。

</details>


### [20] [Tuning-free Instruction-based Video Editing Via Structural Noise Initialization and Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15533)
*Song Wu,Xinyu Chen,Qian Wang,Liang Li,Zili Yi,Junlan Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免调优、基于指令的视频编辑框架，通过结构性噪声初始化策略（SNIS）和噪声引导机制（NGM），利用噪声潜空间中的丰富信息，实现高质量、一致的视频编辑。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的免调优方法未能充分利用噪声潜空间中嵌入的丰富信息，导致视频编辑效果不理想。

Method: 提出结构性噪声初始化策略（SNIS），对编辑区域分配更高噪声（促进内容变化）、对未编辑区域分配更低噪声（保持内容一致）；引入噪声引导机制（NGM），利用生成模型中的视频先验，整合噪声潜空间中的信息指导去噪过程。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在视觉质量上更优，达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 从噪声潜空间角度出发，通过SNIS和NGM可有效提升免调优视频编辑的质量和一致性。

Original Abstract: Video editing poses a significant challenge. While a series of tuning-free methods circumvent the need for extensive data collection and model training, they often underutilize the rich information embedded within noisy latent, leading to unsatisfactory results. To address this, we propose a \textit{tuning-free, instruction-based} video editing framework. We approach video editing from the perspective of noisy latent: we design a Structural Noise Initialization Strategy (SNIS) to secure a superior editing starting point by assigning higher noise levels to edited regions (to facilitate content change) and lower noise levels to unedited regions (to maintain content consistency). We introduce a Noise Guidance Mechanism (NGM), which leverages the video prior in the generative model and effectively integrates rich information within the noisy latent to guide the denoising process, thereby preserving unedited content and overall visual coherence. Experiments show that our proposed method achieves better visual quality and state-of-the-art performance.

中文摘要翻译: 视频编辑是一个重大挑战。尽管一系列免调优方法避免了大量数据收集和模型训练的需求，但它们常常未能充分利用嵌入在噪声潜空间中的丰富信息，导致结果不尽如人意。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了一种免调优、基于指令的视频编辑框架。我们从噪声潜空间的角度来处理视频编辑：我们设计了结构性噪声初始化策略（SNIS），通过为编辑区域分配更高的噪声水平（以促进内容变化）和为未编辑区域分配更低的噪声水平（以保持内容一致性），从而获得更优的编辑起点。我们引入了噪声引导机制（NGM），该机制利用生成模型中的视频先验，有效整合噪声潜空间中的丰富信息来指导去噪过程，从而保持未编辑内容及整体的视觉连贯性。实验表明，我们提出的方法取得了更好的视觉质量和最先进的性能。

</details>


### [21] [Learning Dynamic Structural Specialization for Underwater Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15535)
*Lin Hong,Chenhui Wang,Linan Deng,Yuning Cui,Yu Zhang,Xin Wang,Bojian Zhang,Wenqi Ren,Xingchen Yang,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DSS-USOD，一种基于动态结构特化的RGB水下显著目标检测方法，通过将共享基础表征分解为边界敏感和区域一致两个分支，并利用空间协调模块动态调节其贡献，从而在退化水下图像中实现精准的显著目标检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有水下显著目标检测方法在水下图像退化条件下常出现目标定位不准确、显著区域碎片化和边界预测粗糙等问题，亟需一种能同时保持边界精度和区域一致性的检测方法。

Method: DSS-USOD方法首先从单张水下图像中提取共享基础表征，然后将其分解为边界敏感分支（建模细粒度边界细节）和区域一致分支（捕获区域级结构一致性）；通过空间协调模块根据局部结构上下文自适应调节两个分支的相对贡献；并引入协作结构监督促进分支特化与空间协调的稳定。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得了优越性能，并在真实水下机器人平台上验证了其在目标检测任务中的实际有效性。

Conclusion: DSS-USOD通过动态结构特化有效平衡了边界精度与区域一致性，显著提升了退化水下环境中的显著目标检测性能，并具备实际应用价值。

Original Abstract: Underwater salient object detection (USOD) has attracted increasing attention for underwater visual scene understanding and vision-guided robotic applications. However, existing USOD methods still struggle with underwater image degradations, which often lead to inaccurate object localization, fragmented salient regions, and coarse boundary prediction. To address these challenges, this paper proposes DSS-USOD, a novel RGB-based USOD method built upon dynamic structural specialization. DSS-USOD extracts a shared base representation from a single underwater image, decomposes it into boundary-sensitive and region-coherent structural features, and dynamically coordinates their contributions according to local structural context. Specifically, the extracted shared base representation is decomposed into a boundary-sensitive branch for modeling fine-grained boundary details and a region-coherent branch for capturing region-level structural consistency. A spatial coordination module is then introduced to adaptively regulate the relative contributions of the two branches according to local structural context. Moreover, cooperative structural supervision is introduced to promote branch specialization and stabilize spatial coordination, enabling DSS-USOD to better balance boundary precision and region coherence under degraded underwater conditions. Extensive experiments show that DSS-USOD achieves superior performance on benchmark datasets. Finally, real-world deployment on an underwater robot validates the practical effectiveness of DSS-USOD for underwater object inspection.

中文摘要翻译: 水下显著目标检测（USOD）在水下视觉场景理解和视觉引导机器人应用中受到越来越多关注。然而，现有的USOD方法仍难以应对水下图像退化问题，这常常导致目标定位不准确、显著区域碎片化以及边界预测粗糙。为应对这些挑战，本文提出DSS-USOD，一种基于动态结构特化的新型RGB水下显著目标检测方法。DSS-USOD从单张水下图像中提取共享基础表征，将其分解为边界敏感和区域一致的结构特征，并根据局部结构上下文动态协调它们的贡献。具体而言，提取的共享基础表征被分解为一个用于建模细粒度边界细节的边界敏感分支和一个用于捕获区域级结构一致性的区域一致分支；随后引入空间协调模块，根据局部结构上下文自适应地调节两个分支的相对贡献。此外，引入协作结构监督以促进分支特化并稳定空间协调，使DSS-USOD能够在退化水下条件下更好地平衡边界精度与区域一致性。大量实验表明，DSS-USOD在基准数据集上取得了优越性能。最后，在水下机器人上的实际部署验证了DSS-USOD在水下目标检测中的实际有效性。

</details>


### [22] [RoiMAM: Region-of-Interest Medical Attention Model for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15561)
*Jiayan Yang,Zhuoyu Wu,Wenqi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RoiMAM，一个高效视觉-语言模型，通过训练自由的ROI生成模块和语义选择性抑制机制聚焦病灶区域，结合文本提示增强器提供模态特定上下文，在不到MedVInT-TD模型20%参数量的情况下，在SLAKE和PMC-VQA上分别提升约2%和4.6%的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MedVQA模型依赖大型架构和闭集答案，限制了效率和临床适用性，需要更高效、准确的解决方案。

Method: 提出了RoiMAM，包含两个核心模块：1) 无需训练的ROI生成模块结合语义选择性抑制，聚焦病灶相关区域；2) 文本提示增强器，在不引入额外训练参数的情况下提供模态特定上下文。

Result: 在不到MedVInT-TD模型20%参数量的条件下，在SLAKE上准确率提升约2%，在PMC-VQA上准确率提升约4.6%。

Conclusion: RoiMAM通过轻量级设计和高效的病灶聚焦机制，在显著降低模型规模的同时提升了MedVQA准确率，验证了高效VLM在临床诊断中的潜力。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) facilitate medical visual question answering (MedVQA) by jointly interpreting images and text. However, existing models typically depend on large architectures and closed-set answers, which limits their efficiency and potential clinical applicability. To overcome these shortcomings, we introduce RoiMAM, an efficient VLM. It integrates a training-free ROI Generation Module with Semantic Selective Suppression to focus on lesion-relevant regions, alongside a Text Prompt Enhancer module that provides modality-specific context without introducing training parameters. Compared to the widely used MedVInT-TD model, our design achieves efficient and accurate diagnosis at less than 20\% of the model size, while improving accuracy by approximately 2% on SLAKE and 4.6% on PMC-VQA.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）通过联合解释图像和文本，促进了医学视觉问答（MedVQA）的发展。然而，现有模型通常依赖大型架构和闭集答案，这限制了其效率和潜在的临床适用性。为克服这些缺陷，我们引入了RoiMAM，一个高效的VLM。它整合了一个无需训练的ROI生成模块，结合语义选择性抑制以聚焦病灶相关区域，以及一个文本提示增强器模块，在不引入训练参数的情况下提供模态特定上下文。与广泛使用的MedVInT-TD模型相比，我们的设计在不到其20%模型尺寸的条件下实现了高效准确的诊断，同时在SLAKE上准确率提升约2%，在PMC-VQA上提升约4.6%。

</details>


### [23] [MI-CXR: A Benchmark for Longitudinal Reasoning over Multi-Interval Chest X-rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15574)
*Sunghwan Steve Cho,Yunseok Han,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MI-CXR基准，用于评估多时序胸部X光片的视觉语言模型推理能力。14个模型平均准确率仅29.3%，揭示模型在时间约束和全局一致性推理上的严重缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有医学VQA基准多聚焦单张图像或短时间隔图像对，缺乏对多就诊时序（多间隔）胸部X光片序列的标准化评估基准，无法衡量模型在临床所需的长期病程演变推理能力。

Method: 构建包含五次就诊时间线的MI-CXR基准，设计三类互补任务：时序事件定位、区间变化推理、全局轨迹总结；评估14个SOTA视觉语言模型，并采用分阶段诊断探测分析模型错误模式。

Result: 14个SOTA VLM平均准确率仅29.3%，仅略高于随机猜测。模型能生成局部合理的区间描述，但无法施加时序约束或跨完整时间线整合证据形成全局一致决策。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在纵向医学推理中存在根本性局限，MI-CXR为评估此类能力提供了原则性基准，揭示了模型在时间约束和全局一致性推理方面的关键不足。

Original Abstract: Longitudinal chest X-ray (CXR) interpretation requires reasoning over disease evolution across multiple patient visits, yet most existing medical VQA benchmarks focus on single images or short-horizon image pairs. We introduce MI-CXR, a benchmark for standardized evaluation of Multi-Interval longitudinal reasoning over multi-visit CXR sequences, without requiring free-form report generation or additional clinical context. MI-CXR comprises five-way multiple-choice questions over five-visit patient timelines and instantiates three complementary task families: Temporal Event Localization, Interval-wise Change Reasoning, and Global Trajectory Summarization, which assess clinically grounded visual reasoning over time. Evaluating 14 state-of-the-art vision-language models (VLMs) shows low overall performance, with an average accuracy of 29.3%, only modestly above random guessing. Using stage-wise diagnostic probing, we find that models often produce locally plausible interval descriptions but fail to enforce temporal constraints or compose evidence into globally consistent decisions over the full timeline. These findings reveal key limitations of current VLMs and establish MI-CXR as a principled benchmark for longitudinal medical reasoning. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/AIDASLab/MI-CXR

中文摘要翻译: 纵向胸部X光片（CXR）判读需要推理跨多次患者就诊的疾病演变过程，然而现有大多数医学VQA基准仅关注单张图像或短时间隔的图像对。我们提出了MI-CXR，这是一个用于标准化评估多就诊CXR序列上的多间隔纵向推理能力的基准，无需自由形式的报告生成或额外临床背景信息。MI-CXR包含基于五次就诊时间线的五选多项选择题，并实例化三类互补任务家族：时序事件定位、区间变化推理和全局轨迹总结，从而评估基于临床的时间维度视觉推理能力。对14个最先进的视觉语言模型（VLM）的评估显示其整体性能较低，平均准确率为29.3%，仅略高于随机猜测。通过分阶段诊断探测，我们发现模型通常能生成局部合理的区间描述，但无法施加时序约束，也无法将证据整合为跨越完整时间线的全局一致决策。这些发现揭示了当前VLM的关键局限性，并将MI-CXR确立为纵向医学推理的原则性基准。该基准发布在https://github.com/AIDASLab/MI-CXR

</details>


### [24] [LDGuid: A Framework for Robust Change Detection via Latent Difference Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15582)
*Jiaxuan Zhao,Ali Bereyhi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LDGuid框架，通过显式学习并注入语义差异信息来增强遥感变化检测模型的性能，在多个基准数据集上验证了其有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的深度学习变化检测模型难以显式表示任务相关的语义差异，导致在复杂场景（如光谱噪声干扰）下性能受限。

Method: 提出LDGuid框架，利用对抗自编码器构建差异嵌入（DE）模块，通过信息瓶颈方法预训练，使其仅学习事件前后样本中与任务相关的差异，然后将学习到的潜在差异作为显式引导信号注入CD模型中。

Result: 将LDGuid集成到U-Net、BIT和AERNet等基线模型中，在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、SVCD和CaBuAr数据集上进行评估。结果表明LDGuid在所有基准上均提升了分割性能，尤其在受光谱噪声影响的挑战性场景中提升显著，并具备融入领域知识（如任务特定光谱指数）的能力。

Conclusion: 语义差异学习能够显著增强遥感变化检测模型的鲁棒性，LDGuid框架为显式引导变化检测提供了有效范式。

Original Abstract: Modern deep learning models for change detection (CD) often struggle to explicitly represent task-relevant semantic differences. This paper proposes the Latent Difference Guidance (LDGuid) framework that explicitly learns and injects semantic differences into CD models. LDGuid deploys adversarial autoencoding to implement a difference embedding (DE) module. The DE module is pretrained via the information bottleneck method, restricting it to learn only task-relevant differences between pre- and post-event samples. The learned latent difference is then used as an explicit guidance signal in the CD model. We validate LDGuid by integrating it into U-Net, BIT, and AERNet baselines for CD and evaluating it on LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, SVCD, and CaBuAr datasets. Experimental results show that LDGuid enhances segmentation performance across all benchmarks, with particularly remarkable gains in challenging settings affected by spectral noise. The results further highlight the ability of LDGuid in incorporating domain knowledge, such as task-specific spectral indices. Our findings suggest that semantic difference learning can drastically enhance the robustness of CD in remote sensing.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的用于变化检测（CD）的现代深度学习模型通常难以显式表示与任务相关的语义差异。本文提出了潜在差异引导（LDGuid）框架，该框架显式学习语义差异并将其注入CD模型中。LDGuid部署对抗自编码器来实现差异嵌入（DE）模块。该DE模块通过信息瓶颈方法进行预训练，限制其仅学习事件前后样本中与任务相关的差异。随后，学习到的潜在差异被用作CD模型中的显式引导信号。我们将LDGuid集成到U-Net、BIT和AERNet等CD基线模型中，并在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、SVCD和CaBuAr数据集上进行了评估。实验结果表明，LDGuid在所有基准上都提升了分割性能，尤其在受光谱噪声影响的挑战性场景中取得了显著提升。结果进一步凸显了LDGuid融入领域知识（如任务特定光谱指数）的能力。我们的研究结果表明，语义差异学习可以显著增强遥感变化检测的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [25] [Unsupervised 3D Human Pose Estimation via Conditional Multi-view Ancestral Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15583)
*Ryohei Goto,Takuya Fujihashi,Shunsuke Saruwatari,Fumio Okura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需3D监督的单视图3D人体姿态估计方法，利用预训练的2D运动扩散模型先验，通过条件多视图祖先采样（cMAS）优化3D姿态，在瑜伽数据集上达到跨域SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有3D人体姿态估计方法严重依赖3D标注数据，而在极端姿态或跨域场景下获取3D监督困难。作者希望利用丰富的2D姿态扩散先验来引导3D姿态估计，从而摆脱对3D标注的依赖。

Method: 提出条件多视图祖先采样（cMAS），将扩散模型的多视图祖先采样扩展到2D-3D姿态提升任务。该方法在2D运动扩散模型的噪声空间中优化3D姿态，使其多视图投影符合2D扩散先验的流形，同时约束3D姿态与给定的2D姿态及人体解剖学约束一致。

Result: 在瑜伽数据集上，该方法在跨域场景下优于现有的有监督和无监督3D姿态估计方法，尤其是在缺乏3D监督的极端人体姿态上表现更佳。

Conclusion: 利用2D扩散先验可以有效实现无需3D监督的单视图3D人体姿态估计，为极端姿态和跨域场景下的3D姿态估计提供了一种新范式。

Original Abstract: We propose a method of estimating a 3D human pose from a single view without 3D supervision. The key to our method is to leverage the 2D diffusion priors of motion diffusion models (MDMs) pre-trained on large 2D human pose datasets. Specifically, we extend multi-view ancestral sampling of diffusion models to the task of 2D-3D lifting of human pose. To this end, we newly propose a conditional multi-view ancestral sampling (cMAS) that optimizes the 3D pose such that its multi-view projections follow the manifold in 2D MDM noise space, while conditioning the 3D pose to match the given 2D poses and anatomical constraints of humans. Experiments on the Yoga dataset demonstrate that our method achieves better cross-domain performance compared to state-of-the-art supervised and unsupervised 3D pose estimation methods, including extreme human poses where 3D supervision is unavailable. Code is available at: https://github.com/asaa0001/c-MAS.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出一种无需3D监督即可从单视图估计3D人体姿态的方法。该方法的关键在于利用在大规模2D人体姿态数据集上预训练的运动扩散模型（MDMs）所包含的2D扩散先验。具体而言，我们将扩散模型的多视图祖先采样扩展到人体姿态的2D-3D提升任务中。为此，我们新提出了一种条件多视图祖先采样（cMAS），该方法在2D运动扩散模型噪声空间中优化3D姿态，使其多视图投影遵循该流形，同时约束3D姿态与给定的2D姿态及人体解剖学约束相匹配。在瑜伽数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在跨域性能上优于现有最先进的有监督和无监督3D姿态估计方法，包括在缺乏3D监督的极端人体姿态场景下。代码已开源：https://github.com/asaa0001/c-MAS。

</details>


### [26] [Efficient Image Synthesis with Sphere Latent Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15592)
*Tung Do,Thuan Hoang Nguyen,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦的球面编码器框架，将固定的预训练图像编码器与单独的潜在去噪模型分离，在球面潜在空间中完成训练，避免了像素空间与潜在空间间的反复转换，提升了计算效率和生成质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有少步生成方法（如一致性模型、均值流方法）存在训练不稳定和可扩展性有限的问题；Sphere Encoder虽能少步生成高质量图像，但需在推理时反复在像素空间和潜在空间间转换，并在单一架构中联合优化重建与生成，导致计算效率低下且存在重建与生成的目标冲突。

Method: 将框架解耦为固定的预训练图像编码器和单独的潜在去噪模型，后者完全在球面潜在空间中训练；消除训练和推理过程中重复的像素空间操作，使重建与生成能够独立专化。

Result: 在Animal-Faces、Oxford-Flowers和ImageNet-1K数据集上，该方法在生成质量和推理速度上显著优于Sphere Encoder，并与强少步和多步基线方法取得竞争性结果。

Conclusion: 解耦框架有效解决了Sphere Encoder的计算效率问题和目标冲突，提升了少步图像生成的性能与可扩展性。

Original Abstract: Few-step image generation has seen rapid progress, with consistency and meanflow-based methods significantly reducing the number of sampling steps. Despite their low inference cost, these approaches often suffer from training instability and limited scalability. Sphere Encoder is a recent alternative that produces high-quality images in only a few steps; however, it requires repeated transitions between the pixel space and latent space during inference while jointly optimizing reconstruction and generation within a single architecture. This design leads to computational inefficiency and objective conflict between reconstruction and generation. To address these limitations, we decouple the framework into a fixed pretrained image encoder and a separate latent denoising model trained entirely in a spherical latent space. Our approach eliminates repeated pixel-space operations during training and inference, improving efficiency and allowing reconstruction and generation to specialize independently. On Animal-Faces, Oxford-Flowers and ImageNet-1K datasets, our method significantly outperforms Sphere Encoder in both generation quality and inference speed, while achieving competitive results against strong few-step and multi-step baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 少步图像生成取得了快速进展，其中一致性模型和均值流方法显著减少了采样步数。尽管推理成本低，这些方法常面临训练不稳定和可扩展性有限的问题。Sphere Encoder是一种近期的替代方案，能够在仅少数几步中生成高质量图像；然而，它在推理过程中需要在像素空间和潜在空间之间反复转换，同时在单一架构内联合优化重建和生成。这种设计导致计算效率低下以及重建与生成之间的目标冲突。为解决这些局限，我们将框架解耦为一个固定的预训练图像编码器和一个单独的潜在去噪模型，后者完全在球面潜在空间中训练。我们的方法消除了训练和推理过程中重复的像素空间操作，提高了效率，并使重建和生成能够各自独立专化。在Animal-Faces、Oxford-Flowers和ImageNet-1K数据集上，我们的方法在生成质量和推理速度方面均显著优于Sphere Encoder，同时在与强少步和多步基线方法的对比中取得了具有竞争力的结果。

</details>


### [27] [Latent Video Prediction Learns Better World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15618)
*Ali J Alrasheed,Aryan Yazdan Parast,Basim Azam,James Bailey,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估了四个前沿视频基础模型（V-JEPA 2.1、V-JEPA 2、VideoPrism、VideoMAEv2）在五个鲁棒性维度上的表现，发现潜在预测模型在所有维度上形成独特且一致的优越性能剖面，为鲁棒世界建模提供了有力证据。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自监督视频模型虽被越来越多地视为世界模型，但其评估仍局限于干净基准上的单一top-1准确率，无法揭示其作为世界模型的真正潜力，存在重大评估缺口。

Method: 选取四个容量匹配的前沿视频基础模型（V-JEPA 2.1、V-JEPA 2、VideoPrism、VideoMAEv2），在五个与视频世界模型部署相关的鲁棒性轴上进行系统评估：特征判别性、损坏鲁棒性、细粒度判别、遮挡鲁棒性和时间方向敏感性。

Result: 潜在预测模型在所有五个轴上均表现出独特且一致的优越性：在像素损坏下退化更优雅、遮挡下保留可用类别结构而非仅是几何稳定性、捕捉细粒度物理接触线索、独特编码时间箭头。冻结V-JEPA 2骨干加轻量注意力探针在损坏和遮挡鲁棒性上优于全微调VideoMAE和有监督TimeSformer。

Conclusion: 实验结果提供了支持潜在预测用于鲁棒世界建模的具体新证据。

Original Abstract: Self-supervised video models are increasingly framed as world models, yet their evaluation remains largely confined to a single top-1 accuracy score on clean benchmarks. This leaves a major gap in comprehending their potential as world models. We present the first systematic study addressing this gap, analyzing four matched-capacity frontier video foundation models, V-JEPA 2.1, V-JEPA 2, VideoPrism, and VideoMAEv2, across five robustness axes relevant to their deployment as video world models: feature discriminability, corruption robustness, fine-grained discrimination, occlusion robustness, and sensitivity to temporal direction. Our evaluations establish that across all five axes, latent-prediction models form a distinct and consistent profile. They degrade more gracefully under pixel corruption, preserve usable class structure rather than mere geometric stability under occlusion, capture fine-grained physical contact cues without reconstructing pixels, and uniquely encode the arrow of time. These advantages can even survive task adaptation: a frozen V-JEPA 2 backbone with a lightweight attentive probe outperforms a fully fine-tuned VideoMAE and a supervised TimeSformer on corruption and occlusion robustness. Our extensive results offer concrete new evidence in favor of latent prediction for robust world modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 自监督视频模型日益被构建为世界模型，但其评估仍主要局限于干净基准上的单一top-1准确率分数。这留下了理解它们作为世界模型潜力的重大缺口。我们提出了首个填补该缺口的系统性研究，分析了四个容量匹配的前沿视频基础模型——V-JEPA 2.1、V-JEPA 2、VideoPrism和VideoMAEv2，在与其作为视频世界模型部署相关的五个鲁棒性轴上：特征判别性、损坏鲁棒性、细粒度判别、遮挡鲁棒性和时间方向敏感性。我们的评估确定，在所有五个轴上，潜在预测模型形成了独特且一致的剖面。它们在像素损坏下退化更优雅，在遮挡下保留可用的类别结构而非仅仅是几何稳定性，在不重建像素的情况下捕捉细粒度物理接触线索，并独特地编码时间箭头。这些优势甚至能在任务适应中存活：一个带有轻量注意力探针的冻结V-JEPA 2骨干在损坏和遮挡鲁棒性上优于全微调的VideoMAE和有监督的TimeSformer。我们广泛的实验结果提供了支持潜在预测用于鲁棒世界建模的具体新证据。

</details>


### [28] [LRCP: Low-Rank Compressibility Guided Visual Token Pruning for Efficient LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15621)
*Hongyu Lu,Feng Zhang,Wenwei Jin,Huanling Hu,Tianjun Shi,Shikai Jiang,Yao Hu,Jiawei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LRCP框架，利用视觉 token 表示的低秩结构，通过 PCA 估计主成分子空间并基于投影残差评分实现无训练 token 压缩，在极高压缩比下保持优异的多模态理解性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于注意力的 token 重要性估计存在位置偏差，基于表示的方法忽略视觉 token 集的全局结构。作者从低秩可压缩性视角出发，发现视觉 token 具有显著低秩结构且主成分子空间在随机移除大量 token 后仍保持稳定，由此提出更优的压缩方法。

Method: 提出 LRCP 无训练压缩框架：首先通过 PCA 估计视觉 token 的低秩主成分子空间，然后计算每个 token 在该子空间上的投影残差作为评分，保留那些无法被低秩背景很好解释的 token（即投影残差大的 token），从而压缩视觉 token 数量。

Result: 在图像理解任务中以88.9%的 token 压缩率保持原性能的94.7%；在视频理解任务中以87.5%的 token 压缩率保持平均准确率的97.8%。

Conclusion: 视觉 token 表示具有显著的低秩可压缩性，基于 PCA 投影残差评分的 LRCP 框架能高效识别信息量大的 token，在极高压缩比下仍能保持多模态理解性能，是一种训练免费的通用视觉 token 压缩方案。

Original Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) achieve strong multimodal understanding, but their inference cost grows rapidly with the number of visual tokens, especially for high-resolution images and long videos. Existing attention-based methods estimate token importance from attention scores, which may introduce positional bias, while representation-based methods reduce visual redundancy based on feature relations or reconstruction errors, overlooking the global structure of the visual token set. In this paper, we revisit visual token compression from the perspective of low-rank compressibility. Across models and datasets, we observe that visual token representations exhibit a pronounced low-rank structure, with a dominant subspace that remains stable even after a large fraction of tokens is randomly removed. Motivated by this finding, we propose LRCP, a training-free compression framework that first estimates the dominant low-rank subspace of visual tokens via PCA, and then scores each token by its projection residual onto this subspace, retaining tokens that are poorly explained by the low-rank background. Extensive experiments show that LRCP achieves superior results, preserving 94.7% of the original image-understanding performance with an 88.9% token reduction and 97.8% of the average video-understanding accuracy with an 87.5% token reduction.

中文摘要翻译: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）具有强大的多模态理解能力，但其推理成本随着视觉 token 数量的增加而迅速增长，尤其是在高分辨率图像和长视频场景中。现有基于注意力的方法通过注意力分数估计 token 重要性，但可能引入位置偏差；而基于表示的方法则根据特征关系或重建误差减少视觉冗余，忽略了视觉 token 集的全局结构。本文从低秩可压缩性的角度重新审视视觉 token 压缩问题。跨模型和数据集，我们观察到视觉 token 表示具有显著的低秩结构，其主成分子空间即使在随机移除大量 token 后仍保持稳定。受此发现启发，我们提出 LRCP，一种无需训练即可使用的压缩框架。该框架首先通过 PCA 估计视觉 token 的低秩主成分子空间，然后根据每个 token 在该子空间上的投影残差进行评分，保留那些无法被低秩背景很好解释的 token。大量实验表明，LRCP 取得了优越的效果：在图像理解任务中，以 88.9% 的 token 压缩率保持了原始性能的 94.7%；在视频理解任务中，以 87.5% 的 token 压缩率保持了平均准确率的 97.8%。

</details>


### [29] [Learning Disentangled Representations for Generalized Multi-view Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15640)
*Xin Zou,Ruimeng Liu,Chang Tang,Zhenglai Li,Xinwang Liu,Kunlun He,Wanqing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出广义多视图自编码器（GMAE），通过解耦表示学习和互信息调控解决多视图聚类中的视图-分布纠缠问题，在13个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度多视图聚类方法在跨视图融合时存在视图-分布纠缠问题，导致共享隐空间质量下降，聚类结构不清晰。

Method: 采用双路自编码器将源特征解耦为视图特定和视图公共嵌入；构建跨视图对抗判别器引导视图特定编码器捕获更具区分性的特征；通过调控互信息对齐分布并防止表示坍塌。

Result: 在13个基准数据集上，GMAE在完整和不完整多视图聚类任务中均一致优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: GMAE通过解耦表示学习有效保持了跨视图互补性，提升了聚类结构质量，并在多个基准上验证了其优越性。

Original Abstract: Multi-View Clustering (MVC) has gained significant attention for its ability to leverage complementary information across diverse views. However, existing deep MVC methods often struggle with view-distribution entanglement during cross-view fusion, which hampers the quality of the shared latent space and leads to suboptimal Figures. To address this issue, we propose the Generalized Multi-view Auto-Encoder (GMAE), a framework designed to preserve cross-view complementarity through disentangled representation learning. Specifically, GMAE employs dual-path autoencoders to decouple source features into view-specific and view-common embeddings, facilitating the discovery of clearer clustering structures. We further construct cross-view adversarial discriminators to guide view-specific encoders in capturing more discriminative features. By strategically modulating mutual information, GMAE effectively aligns distributions and prevents representation collapse, ensuring the generation of robust, non-trivial embeddings. Comprehensive experiments on 13 benchmark datasets demonstrate that GMAE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both complete and incomplete MVC tasks. Our code implementation is available at the repository: https://github.com/obananas/GMAE.

中文摘要翻译: 多视图聚类（MVC）因其能够利用不同视图间的互补信息而受到广泛关注。然而，现有的深度MVC方法在跨视图融合过程中常常面临视图-分布纠缠的问题，这损害了共享隐空间的质量，并导致次优的聚类结构。为解决这一问题，我们提出了广义多视图自编码器（GMAE），一个通过解耦表示学习来保持跨视图互补性的框架。具体而言，GMAE采用双路自编码器将源特征解耦为视图特定嵌入和视图公共嵌入，从而促进更清晰的聚类结构的发现。我们进一步构建了跨视图对抗判别器，引导视图特定编码器捕获更具区分性的特征。通过战略性地调控互信息，GMAE有效地对齐了分布并防止了表示坍塌，确保了鲁棒且非平凡的嵌入的生成。在13个基准数据集上的全面实验表明，GMAE在完整和不完整多视图聚类任务中均一致地优于最先进的方法。我们的代码实现可在以下仓库获取：https://github.com/obananas/GMAE。

</details>


### [30] [ChronoEarth-492K: A Large Scale and Long Horizon Spatiotemporal Hyperspectral Earth Observation Dataset and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15666)
*Haozhe Si,Yuxuan Wan,Yuqing Wang,Minh Do,Han Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChronoEarth-492K，第一个大规模、时间校准的高光谱自监督学习数据集，基于NASA EO-1 Hyperion任务（2001-2017）构建，包含492,354个全球补丁，并配套ChronoEarth-Benchmark基准测试套件，支持静态、短时域和长时域时间任务，为时空调高光谱表征学习提供了系统平台。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有高光谱自监督学习数据集在时间维度上较浅，缺乏长时间跨度的时空建模能力，限制了高光谱遥感在土地覆盖、生态系统动态等任务中的时间分析研究。

Method: 基于NASA EO-1 Hyperion任务（2001-2017）构建ChronoEarth-492K数据集，包含492,354个辐射校准补丁，覆盖全球185,398个地点，其中28,786个地点具有多时相序列；在此基础上建立ChronoEarth-Benchmark评估套件，涵盖静态、短时域和长时域任务，并使用六个开源地理空间产品进行标注。

Result: 构建了首个大规模、时间校准的高光谱SSL数据集，并提供了多种先进高光谱基础模型的广泛基线结果。

Conclusion: ChronoEarth及其基准测试为系统性的时空高光谱表征学习提供了首个大规模、时间基础的平台，填补了高光谱SSL领域长时间跨度数据集的空白。

Original Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) provides dense spectral information for the Earth's surface, enabling material-level understanding of land cover and ecosystem dynamics. Despite recent progress in hyperspectral self-supervised learning (SSL), existing datasets remain temporally shallow, limiting the development of long-horizon spatiotemporal modeling. To address this gap, we introduce ChronoEarth-492K, the first large-scale, temporally calibrated hyperspectral SSL dataset built upon NASA's EO-1 Hyperion mission, the world's longest continuous hyperspectral archive up to date (2001-2017). ChronoEarth-492K comprises 492,354 radiometrically harmonized patches across 185,398 global locations over 17 years, with 28,786 sites containing multi-temporal sequences ($\geq 3$ observations) that enable both short- and long-horizon temporal analysis. Building on this foundation, we establish the ChronoEarth-Benchmark, a unified evaluation suite spanning static, short-horizon, and long-horizon temporal tasks, constructed from six open-source geospatial products covering land cover, crop type, forest dynamics, and soil properties. We further introduce a standardized evaluation protocol and report extensive baseline results across state-of-the-art hyperspectral foundation models. Together, ChronoEarth and benchmark provide the first large-scale, temporally grounded platform for systematic spatiotemporal hyperspectral representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 高光谱成像（HSI）为地球表面提供了密集的光谱信息，能够实现土地覆盖和生态系统动态的材料级理解。尽管高光谱自监督学习（SSL）近期取得了进展，但现有数据集在时间维度上仍然较浅，限制了长时域时空建模的发展。为填补这一空白，我们提出了ChronoEarth-492K——首个大规模、时间校准的高光谱SSL数据集，基于NASA的EO-1 Hyperion任务构建，该任务是目前世界上最长的连续高光谱存档（2001-2017年）。ChronoEarth-492K包含492,354个辐射校准的影像块，覆盖全球185,398个地点，时间跨度为17年，其中28,786个地点包含多时相序列（≥3次观测），支持短时域和长时域时间分析。在此基础上，我们建立了ChronoEarth-Benchmark，这是一个统一的评估套件，涵盖静态、短时域和长时域时间任务，由六个涵盖土地覆盖、作物类型、森林动态和土壤属性的开源地理空间产品构建。我们进一步引入了标准化的评估协议，并报告了跨多种先进高光谱基础模型的广泛基线结果。ChronoEarth及其基准测试共同为系统性的时空高光谱表征学习提供了首个大规模、时间基础平台。

</details>


### [31] [VLMs Trace Without Tracking: Diagnosing Failures in Visual Path Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15672)
*Hyesoo Hong,Minsoo Kim,Wonje Jeung,Sangyeon Yoon,Dongjae Jeon,Albert No*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视觉-语言模型(VLM)在基本视觉操作——线条追踪任务中表现脆弱，即使是最先进的模型也容易因局部相似干扰物而丢失目标路径，且模型缩放、推理提示等标准方法无法有效解决这一问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLM在多模态基准测试中表现强劲，但可能缺乏对基本视觉操作的鲁棒控制。作者希望系统性地研究VLM在"线条追踪"这一基础视觉能力上的表现，揭示其失败机制。

Method: 设计了受控的线条追踪任务，通过引入近邻竞争路径并减少语义和拓扑歧义（如交叉和重叠）来隔离线条追踪能力。结合行为干预和内部分析研究模型失败原因。

Result: (1) 即使最先进的VLM也频繁丢失目标路径，切换到近邻替代路径；(2) 失败源于局部竞争：相似干扰物将模型从正确延续路径拉走；(3) 模型规模扩展收益有限；(4) 推理只能通过代价高昂的替代策略部分补偿；(5) 显式追踪指令无效；(6) 在复杂真实场景（缠结电缆、地铁图）中同样存在路径切换失败问题。

Conclusion: VLM在线条追踪这一基本视觉操作中存在根本性缺陷，局部竞争是核心瓶颈，标准补救措施无法稳定解决该问题。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on multimodal benchmarks, but may still lack robust control over basic visual operations.
